AGANCHENGANI 
“Kusikni Bidingo Seanirang”- ko anga essayni bimango sea. 
Ia tom chimonggipao seanirang mingchikking donga. Seani- 
rangoni mitamrangde baditaba bilsirangni gisepo ong-atgipa 
seminarrangna angni on-gilgiparang onga; mitamara ia Ki-tapna 
semanchagiparang. Ia seanirang Ku sikni (Language) bidingo 
ong a, sea-jotani (Literature) bidingkode man-chapatja. Ia 
seanirangko dakmitingo anga David Abercrombieni ki-tap 
“Problems and Principles in Language Study”, Garo Grammar 
Ki-taprang aro Robins Burlingni Achikku Grammami bidingo 
segipa kitaprangko niaha aro jakkalaha. 
(i) “Ku-sikko Jakkalani” essayo, Ku sikara maia aro songsarni 
gita manderangni janggi tanganio uni kamara maia uko nia. (ii) 
“Phonetics”- ni gimin seanio, A-chikkuni sound unitrangni ba 
gam-ani tokrangni gimin nia, indiba ian kosakgilgil nianisan 
ong aia; sandfrikkite seanio gitade ning tuani dongja. (iii) ‘A- - 
chikkuo Katta Rikani aro Bariani” essayo, Achikkuo kattana 
kangalgijanioni a-bachenge, A-chikku kattarangko tom- - 
chimongna a-bachenganiko janapjole, A chikkuo katta rikna mai 
chol-bewalrangko jakkala aro mai cholrangchi A chikku kattarang 
baridapa, uko nia. (iv) “A chikkuo ‘Raka’ (■) Jakkalani” ingipa 
seanio, ‘Raka’- ni mongsongani aro A chikkuo seanio gelna

man-gijagipa bak ong-aniko nia. Chasong gitalni bi-sarangoni 
bang-aan badia kattao aro kattani badia bako ‘Raka’ totna nanga 
talja. A-chik bi-sarangko iako ma-sie ra-atania ku-tangko 
jakkalanio namdapatani ong-a. (v) ‘A chikku Grammar Ki-taprang” 
segimin ki taprangko nipile, uarangni mongsonggipa niksenggipa 
bakrangko niani ong a. (vi) “Garo Grammarni Bimango Gipin 
Grammarni Bilmanani” essayo, Garo Grammarko segiparang 
English Grammarko name poraigiparang ong-ani gimin aro 
mitam segiparangara English ku-ko aganmanchagiparang ong-ani 
a-sel, Garo Grammarko seania English Grammarni niamrangehi 
aditan japindapaniko man-a ine mesokna jotton ka-a. (vii) 
“A-chikkuo Verb Jakkalani Adita Niamrang” essayo, sentenceo 
Verbni biap, sentenceo Verb jakkalani aro jakkalgijani aro uarang 
gitako nia. (viii) ‘A-chikkuo Verbrangni Tense mo Aspect” ingipa 
seanio, Tenseo daka-ka-ani Verbni bimang aro 
(i) 
mesokmanchaenggipa somoe dingtanggrikna man-aia ine mesoka. 
(ix) “English aro Achikku Tense Systemko Tosusaani” ingipa 
seanio, Englisho jakkalgipa badia Tenserangko A chikkuoba 
jakkala aro badiarangko jakkalna ke-ja uarangko tosusae nia. (x) 
“Ku-amani Bilmananichi Ku gipinko Skie Raanio Gualanirang” 
essayo, Englishko skie ra-gipa A-chik bi-sarang maikai A chikku 
Grammarni niamrangni bilmananichi Englisho seanio badiaba 
gualanirangko daka aro indonga gualanirangko uisamsoe ra-aniara 
mai namgniko on-a, uarangko nia. 
A chikkuoVerb Jakkalani Niamrang, A chikkuo Verbrangni 
Tense aro Aspect aro English aro A chikku Tense Systemko 
Tosusaani-ia minggittam seanirango nangrimgrikani baksana 
apsanangani bang-a. Verbantinin Tense donga aro Tenseko 
talatnade Verb gri cholija; ia minggni ma-kaen nangrimgrika. 
English Grammarni Tense Systemko ja-rike seaniko Garo Grammar 
Ki-taprango nika. Ia ku-sik minggnini Grammarni niamrangko 
ma-ekatgija aro ma ekateba skie ra anichi, uarangko nambate 
ma-sina man-a. Indake ia katta bichong minggittam stitgrikani 
gimin, mitam paragraphrang, sentencerang aro mesokanirangko

ra-grikpilaia. Katta bichong ge-sao segiminko ma-siode, 
gipinrangko ma-sianio nengrabata. Name niode, badia katta 
bichong Grammarni badia bakna mikpakma nia uina man-a. 
Kusikni Niamrang ba Grammarkode “gisik nangatani grigipa, 
ran-krekgipa katta bichong” ine mitamrang agana. Indiba 
ku-tangni gimin uie ra anio gisik nangani donggijan ong-na man-ja. 
Ku-tangko uiani aro uni Grammarni niamrangko name tale 
ma-sianiara sea-jotani bidingo re-gelna man gijagipa ong-a. 
Grammarni niamrangko ja-rikgija seode, golpo, rochona, songsami 
golpo, je seaniko seoba, uarangni me-suaniko gimaatgen, maina 
ma-siani dongjaode, poraie gisik an-sengani dongja. Poedo 
seaniosa poedo segiparangna jakgitelaniko on-gipa ‘poetic licence’ 
donga, jeko jakkalen, poedo segiparang grammarni niamrangko 
gelna choljoka. 
Poedorang aro indaka seanirangko bregiparang aro 
poraigiparang komibea, indiba indakgipa seanirang dongna 
nangchongmota ine angko didigiparangko anga gisik ra-a aro 
uamangna iano mittelaniko on-a. Iano seanirang rama o-rokanisan 
ong-bataia, indiba iarang sanaba uidapaniko, A chikkuni gimin 
ma sidapaniko aro ia seanirangna ning tubate aro talbate 
seanirangko dakna ansaoataniko on-ode, seanirang 
onggramaijawa. 
Angni ia seanirangna anga Burlingni kitaprangko, R.G. 
Mominni ki-tapko, Clearson Sangmani Achik Grammar Ki-tapko 
aro Rev. Remani Mandi Grammar ki-tapko Dr. Milton S. 
Sangmaoniko manaha. Anga bijaoba,ua angna ia ki-taprang uo 
donga ine agane angna uarangko ra srona on aha. la Ki tapna 
Agandapaniko senaba Dr. Sangma jechakjaha. Iarangna anga 
angni mittelpilaniko Dr. Sangmana paraka. Indiba angni 
mittelpilna nangbatgipara Dr. Sangmani angko ki tap sena 
didiatani aro an-saoatanian. Mittelbea, Dr. Sangma. 
Dated Tura, 
The 1st October, 2005. 
Brucellish K. Sangma 
(Renangma)

agAndapani 
Sea-jotaniko dakani nengbegipa kam onga. Jeni gimin sena 
amoba altua katta ong ja. Mikkang kisang chanchichengesa sena 
nanga. Segimin kattani gimin seode, uko dingtangskae sena 
ga-aka. Sechenggipa katta ongode, rama o roke on chenggipa 
gita ongna nanga. Iarangna agreba, Achikkuchi sea katta ramram 
ong*ja. Mongsongbate rakbegipa aro ningtubegipa orto gnanggipa 
kattarangko aro chanchianirangko parakna A chikkuchi neng-a 
aro chuonga kattarangko man janio sena nengbea. Indiba 
A chikrangni skia-poraiani gadangrang tangdororobaengahani 
gimin indakgipa gadangrango poraigiparangna rokom rokom 
seanirangko da o nangbeengaha. 
Ia “Kusikni Bidingo Seanirang”-ko poraie anga kusi ong a, 
maina ian A*chikkuni bidingo chugipa gadangrango 
poraigiparangna dakchakbeani onggen. Iako poraie anga 
indakeba agandapna ska aro namnika. 
Skanggipa, ia Kitapko segipani janapani gita, iani bidingo 
seanirang bilongen komikuenga. Ku sikni bidingo rokom 
rokomrangko uina nangchqngmota, indiba ia uina 
nangchongmotgipaon an ching ja manchakkuenga. 
Gnigipa, a song gipinni aro a songtangni manderang beben 
iani bidingo adita see donangaha, indiba uamang English Grammar 
aro English Ku sikko niatesa A chik Grammarrangko seaha. 
Gittamgipa, A chik Grammarko seon, A chikkuni ganra aro 
aganani bewalrang aro Achikkuoniko examplerangko one seosa 
chong motgipa A chik Grammar ong gen. Uarang da alona 
kingking komikuenga. 
Brigipa, ‘Raka' totani A chikkuni mingsa dingtangbegipa 
Ku sikni bewalko mesoka. Ia Ki tapo iako maina jakkalna 
nangchongmota uko talate on a. 
Je jerangko A chikkuchi seanirango komikuenga ine 
janapahachim, uarangoni mitamko ia Ki tapo kolgapataha ine 
nikna mangen. 
Anga Mrs. B.K. Sangma (Renangma) ko mittelna ska, maina 
ua ia rakbegipa subjectko altuae ma sina man na gita man adipet

(iv) 
seaha. Batanggimin somoerango seanggimin Grammar 
Kitaprangkoba nigopate seaniko dakaha. Uandake ia Kitapko 
poraion iani bidingo badita nambatrorobaaha uko ma sina man a. 
Ia Kitap College aro Universityo poraigiparangna Kusikni 
bewal aro Grammarni bidingo uina dakchakbeani onggen ine 
nikna mana. Iamangna agreba, gipin A chik Kusikni aro 
Grammarni bidingo uina sikgiparangnaba dakchakbeani ong gen 
ineba nikatna man-a. Uandaken ia Ki tapko poraie bang a 
namgnirangko man-gen ine anga ka donga, maina ia Ki-tapko 
segipa an-tangan Literatureni mande ong e, uko skie aro adita 
ki tap aro articlerangko see, name ma-siesa iako seaha. Ian uni 
jatna gimagijagipa donanggimin gam ong a.

(vi) 
Ku-sikko Jakkalani 
Kusikni gimin sandie nianio, ku-sikara maiko daka, ba 
Kuantini kamara maia uko nia. Kusikrangara maia, uarangko 
maidake talatna, pe-rarone, tosusae, mamal dake nina man-gen, 
uni bidingo nianiba ong-a. Una agreba, iara dingtang dingtang 
Kusikrangni form ba bimangni giminba ong-a. 
Ku-sik mandeko songsaro janggi tangna man ata. Chasong 
gitalni Linguistics-o Ku sikko mande sakprakni an tangko 
parakani chol ine ra-gija, songsarni kam mingsa ine raa. 
Matburingrangni gisepo mandesan ku aganaia aro iachin 
tom dake janggi tanggiparangoni mandean nambatgipa 
chubatgipa gadangko ra a. 
Chasong gitalo Ku sikko chanchianirangko parakani chol ine 
ra gija, uko “a means of social control” ba songsarko chalaiani 
bamatani chol inesa raskaa. Kusikara chanchianirangko parakani 
chol ine agannade man-ja. Kbtokni dilgipa doltangni manderangko 
maikoba dakna ba ka na hukum onon, uko chanchiatangko 
paraka ine aganna man-ja. Ian kamrangko name dakat-ka atanisa 
ong-aia. Sal karini gimin golpoode, maming chubata gadangni 
uidapaniko on-ja, ba mandeko seng-batatja, ine David 
Abercrombie agana. Indiba Ku-sikko iandakana jakkalanioba 
songsarni gita gun donga. 
Ku-sik mandeko saksa sakgipinni toko ba dolo 
kratcha grikgija dongtonikata aro meli-nangrimna dakchaka. 
Ku sa ku-gni agangrikani ba golpoaniba mande mandeskako 
ma-siat-uiatgrika aro ripeng baju ong ata. Ramram golpoe 
roanikoba, uni gimin, mande mandeskaming melinangrime 
donganina nangchongmota. 
Kattani ortoni gimin niskana. Kuaganani somoeo agangipani 
mikkango mikrono parakani aro ku-rangni rokomba ku agananina 
ba kattana orto on-a. Katta rakako aganoba, mikrono ka-saani 
chin dongode, knatimgipa ma siaia. Katta chi-oba, ku aganani 
bewalan ua kattani kongbangani aro ning-tugijaniko parakaia 
1

baksana, kattani ortoko dingtangata. 
Agangipa kattako knatimgipani peskaaniba rokomari ortoo 
bon ena mana. “Mai bijak chabaa” ine sing ode, mitamrang 
kadingsmitari “Bijak dongja”, ine neng*mangijan aganchaka. 
Mitamde, dongpajanan sing teke roa ine gisik saa ba kratchaa. 
Mitam jatrangnade indake sing ania mande ragijani aro 
sing enggipani bewal namgijaniko mesokani onga. 
Saoba maikoba aganon, knatimenggipa mande agangipani 
aganako orto peskaa. Kattara maibani gimin ong naba donga. 
Indiba ua kattarang agangipani cholon-bewal aro kam-cholonko 
mesokpilaniba ong na man-a. Re a doani, ganachinani bewalba 
mandeni cholon-bewalko mesoka. Indiba mandeni kam- 
cholonko nikprotbatatgipara uni ku agananian ong aia. Katta 
gam atani, kurangmanchani rokom, be en il engani, jak de ani 
ba moani pilakan agangipani cholon-bewalko parakgipa chinrang 
ong a. Saoba a gittalko grongon, uni kuaganani bewalonikon 
an ching ua mandeni cholon-bewalko uina jotton ka-a. Mande 
gisik namen aganengama, ma katta chroba ning ode ka anangani 
wal sare chadoengama, uni ku aganani bewalan paraka. 
Basakobade, agangipa mande uitelen aro an tangni chong mot 
cholon-bewalko donnue, an tangko gipin dake mesokskanaba, 
ku aganani bewalko dingtangata ba dakjea. Indake mandeni 
cholon-bewalko uni ku aganani bewaloniko chanchichipe 
ra anirang basakobade tik ong ja. 
Ku aganani rokom ba bewal basakobade dal kalgipa tokni 
(language) ning*o chonchonkalgipa tokrangni (dialect) ma*malko 
nakatata. Manderang dol ge sani ning o ong on, ua dolo 
donggiparang pilakan Ku sikni apsan bewalko mesoka. Ia bewalo 
apsan ong aniara, jean gipin dolrangni bewaloni dingtanga, ua 
tokko tange rakkiani chol ong a. Ku*sikni bewalrangan songsarni 
bewalrangko ma kaatgipa aro gugraatgipaba ong-a, ine Ku sikni 
bidingo uibegiparang agana. 
Donnue ba donmitape aganani (concealed/ secret language) 
A chikkuoba donga. Dal drobaenggipa bi*sarangni. u Re*na 
man•jawa ,, inani palo “Ne*ra jan-mawa,” “Namja”-ni palo

“Jamna”, ine aganako basakoba knaa. Skanggipa syllableni (Katta 
tongni) an pagipa consonantko, gnigipa katta tongni an pagipa 
2 
consonantni biapona ra angskaa. Gnigipa tongoniko ragalgipa 
consonantko skanggipa tongni consonantni biapo onjotskaa. 
Skulni bisarang an tangtangni golpoako an-tangtangna 
dalbatgiparangna ba uamangni skigiparangna uiatna sikjaode, 
indake consonantko rajujagrike aganronga. Indiba iandake 
jakkalanio chasong/ tro ba dol/ tok apsangipani ningosa 
ongTonga. Donnugimin Kusikko gipinrangba jakkala, jekai, niam 
pegiparang niamoni jokna. “Bostu dongja” ine aganode, ramram 
knapaaigipade maiba ramram bostuko aganenga inesa 
chanchiaigen. Ian niam pe giparangni dol/ tokode, “un salode 
dakait dakna manjawa ba choljokja” inesa, ba”pekatgipa ringani 
ba samko manbaja” inesa miksongnaba donga. 
Kusikni minggipin kam dongkua. Kusikara mandeko 
mandeskaming nangrimatanisan ongaija, uan mandeko apalni 
agilsakmingba nangrimata. Kusik mandeko uni 
samtangtangming kabinga. Ku’sikni bidingo uibegipa chasong 
gitalni manderangni chanchianio, Ku sikara an tangari 
chanchianisan ong aija, indiba a gilsako wa sae janggi tangani 
chol-rama ba samtangtangko jakkalna man atgipa chol ong a. 
Ku sikni dongimin niamni ning osa mandeni chanchie aro nie- 
nike uie ra aniko pe rarone nia. 
A palni a gilsakni bidingo jakkalna gita Ku sik mingsani 
kattarangko (vocabulary) orto on na gita sulsul name taria aro 
indake tariani bewalko ba orto srongatna donani bimangko 
Englisho semantic structure ine agana. Kattarangko sulsul dongija 
sentenceo jebasi donaiode, miksonganiko talna man*ja. 
Ku*sik ong telgipa a-gilsakchi dokkol ka ako man ja. Uni 
giminan, Ku-sikrangni semantic structure-ba apsan ong ja. Tok/ 
dol apsananggipa, meli-nangrimani donggipa Ku sikrangmangba 
semantic structure-o dingtanggrikaniko mesoka aro ma mal 
dingtangsranggipa Ku sikrangni semantic structure-de apalen 
dingtangsranga.

English gita chugipa gadangni Ku sikrango man gopgipa aro 
mingantina orto ma ekate jakkalgipa kattarang donga. Englisho 
mi-misirangna << crops ,> kattako jakkala, mi aro misinade rice/ 
paddy aro millet kattako jakkala. A chikkuode man-gopgipa katta 
dongja aro indake man gopgipa ortoko on na gita mi aro misi 
katta minggniko jakkaldimna ga aka. Indine inoba, ian 
3 
chu-soksranggipa janapani ongja, maina Englisho ‘we’ kattako 
jakkalgopoba, Achikkuode orto maekate jakkalskaa. Achikkuo, 
agangipa aro knatimgipako man-gopode, anching ine agana. 
Knatimgipako man chapjaode, chinga ine agana. Indake ma ekate 
jakkalgipa gadang chugipa Kusikoba dongskagija ong-na manaia. 
Ku sik mandeko songsaro janggi tangna manata, indiba ua 
maironga songsaro janggi tanga, ua songsarni rokom uni Ku sikni 
kosako namen bilmana. Semantic structure aro social structure 
(songsarni gadang ba bimang) namen nangchapgrika. Ku sikara 
mandeni daka-rikani changa-sapanio nangnikgipa gadangona 
sokani (achievement ) baksan ong-aija, uan ua jatni daka-rikani 
cha&ga-sapani bewalko giproroatgipaba onga. Mitam 
kattarangkode songsarni gadangni krisa ma'siaia. Chukalgipa 
gadangni manderang gisik a-ning bita inon ta Taken masia, indiba 
skia poraia dongpagijagipa songsarni mitam gadangrango ian 
knaronggija katta ongskaa. Ro-ding rodap talate onosa masiatna 
manaigen. 
Semantic structure, social structure-oni ong katgipa 
psychological environmentko (gisikni obosta) mesokasan 
ong aija. Bi*sa uni somoeo jakkalgipa kattarangko man e ra*on, 
dal-droenggipa bi-sa uni samtangtangogipa a-gilsakko nikat- 
niatgipa ma siatgipa rokomari cholrangko man e ra a aro uni 
Ku-sik ia rokomari bilrangni ba cholrangni bilmananiko mesoka. 
Janggi tangani samtangtang mandena golpo ka ani cholrangko 
ba kattarangko on a aro ia kattarang songsarni, toromni aro 
sasonni gita chanchianirangko songni- nokni golporang aro 
chasongni chasongna ripingrorobaenggipa golporang ongatskaa. 
Linguisticsko name poraie sandie nigipa Americani B.L.

Whorf “linguistic relativity” kattako bikotaha. Linguistic 
relativity inon iako mangsonga:Manderangni ku aganani 
bewalrang apsan ong jaode, apsan sakkimangba manderangko 
a gilsakni noksako apsan dake nikatjawa. Biapni aro obostani 
dingtangani kri mitam kattarang badiaba Ku siko dongjaode, 
kugipinni kattarangna orto apsangipa kattarangko man-ja. Jekai, 
A chikkuo bang- a me surangni bimingrang dongoba, carrot, 
beetroot aro capsicumna A chikkuo katta dongja. Uni gimin 
Englishkon jakkaltaina ba Axhikkuo uigimin katta minggni 
minggittamko ra e talatgipe kattarangkosan jakkalna man aia. 
4 
Jekai, capsicum na jalik dal gipa inesa aganrongbataia. Jalik 
katta kugipinonisa ine uioba, uko nrutaonin jakkalengahani 
gimin ua dao uigimin ba jakkalronggipa Achikku katta ongaijok. 
Kusikara sea-jotani cholba onga. Uko name knatoe 
jakkalaniara “literary language” ba sea-jotanio jakkalgipa Kusiko 
ong bata aro ian salanti janggi tanganio jakkalgipa Kusikoni 
bewalan dingtanga. Sea-jotani Kusik inon semanchagipa Kusikko 
mangsongjanaba donga aro ian songsarni chugipa gadangni 
manderangsan jakkalna man aigipaba ongja. Achikkuo mite 
amuao, wanao, grikao, ajeao aro manianirango dingtangsranggipa 
kattarangko jakkala. Ua kattarangko kamalrangna ba mande 
gitchamrangna agre, a dokni gipin manderangde masipaja. 
Ku aganaode ramram kattarangko kandike, sentence 
matchotgijaba aganaia. 
Sea-jotani Ku sikara indiba seanio jakkalgipa Ku sik 
ongTongbata aro uan badiaba a doksani (dialect) Ku sikoniko 
man gipa ong naba donga, jenan manderangni aganronggipa 
Ku sik (Standard Language) ine, maiba ong*gakaianichisarasong 
mikkimko onaha. Sea-jotanio jakkalgipa pilak Ku sikrangan 
aganao jakkalgipa Ku sikrangoni ma eke dingtang ong angaiani 
gimin, atchikape ua Ku-sikko agangipamangba, sea-jotanio 
jakkalgipa Ku sikko bewalde skie ra taipilskana ga aka. Ia 
dingtang ong anganiranga kominaba batnaba donga. Sea-jotanio 
jakkalgipa Ku sik, sea-jotaninasan ong bataioba, ja mano uko je

rokom seaninamangba (jekai, chitti seani, dorkasto galani) seani 
bewal dake ra chaka. Ia ra chakgimin sea-jotanio jakkalgipa 
Ku sik aganna jakkalgipa Ku sikni kosako aditan bilmanronga. 
Uko tik ong e seani bewalrang Ku sikni, ku agangipa ba seanio 
jakkalgipa ong chong, pilak jakkalanirangon ra chakgimin 
bewalrang ba toe nianirang ong baa, aro sea-jotanio ra chakgimin 
bewaloni ekanganirangko ba jakkalsretanirangko raken jegala. 
Jekai, dal batgiparang re baon uamangna chokki on e “Asong” 
ine aganaiode, ua nitoani aro ra chaktoani ong ja, ine uko jegala. 
Donsogimin Grammami niamko ja rikjaoba, uan jegalani ong-gen. 
Jekai, ‘Anga ambin iaosa re angmanaha” . ine aganode, Ku sikni 
niamko ba Grammarni niamko pe ani ong-jok. 
Somoeko mesokgipa adverb “ambin ia” inon 
re mikkangachini somoekosa janapa. Sentenceni verb 
5 
“reangmanaha”- ara batanggimin somoeko mesokgipa ba past 
perfect tenseosa ong a. Somoeko mesokgipa adverbni aro verbni 
tense-ni nangrimgrikani niamko peani gimin, ia sentenceko 
seaoba aganaoba rachakna man ja. 
Poedo seaniosa, songsarni mande ragrikani ba grammarni 
dongimin niamrangko pe ani gnang. Poedo seanio ia dongimin 
ba rachakgimin niamrangko pe oba kema ka aia aro Englisho 
indake dakna on aianikon “poetic licence” ine agana. 
6 
PHONETICS 
(Kiraganao gamanirang, gam-atani aro 
uarangna jakkalgipa chinrangko sulsul niani) 
Saksa sakgipin baksa agangrikanina aiao inmanpilgipa 
cholko bikotanian manderangko namen matburingrangoniko 
dingtangata. Agangrikanina manderangni jakkalgipa chinranga 
rokomgni-knana man gipa aro nikna mangipa chinrang. Englisho 
iarangko Aural aro Visual media ine minga. Dal-giminrangni 
aganako knae uko ku sningesa bi sarang ku aganna abachenga, 
chong motan knana man gipa chinrangko uamang jakkalchenga. 
Unikosa poraina aro senade brsarang skibaa. Chong-motan

skianio knana man gipa chinrangko jakkalna skiani ja*manosa, 
nikna man gipa chinrangkode jakkala. 
Jatantini, sakantini janggi tanganion, agananikosa seanina 
skang jakkala. Seanide agananiko mikronchi nikna man na gita 
tariskaanisa onga. 
Phonetics-ara Aural Medium ba knana man gipa 
agangrikanio jakkalgipa chinrangko sandiani aro talatani ong a. 
Chong motan, mande ku ni gam anirang, uarangko gam atani aro 
gam anirangko knapilskaaniko Phonetics-o man gopa. 
Mandeni ku sika aiao inmanpilbebea. Ku sikantini 
“Linguistic Features” (jekai, Englisho /f/ aro /v/ni gam anirang 
dongoba, Achikkuode ia gamanirang dongja) baksana “Indexical 
Features” donga. Jekai mandeni kusikara, aganna nanggiparangko 
talatna man an baksana, agangipani bilsi, me a ba me chik 
ong ani, mandeni bewal aro jatni bewalko ma sina chinko on a. 
Unbaksanaba, badia jolni mande ukoba uiatna man a. Bi sani 
ku agana mande dal giminni aganaoni dingtangsranga. Me asa 
aganengama mechik aganengama, ukoba anching 
aganenggipako nikgijan talsona man a. Aganako knariken 
agangipara mande ka singipama ka ting dakgipa ukoba uina 
man-a. A chikan aganengama ba gipin jatsa A chikkuo 
aganengama ukoba uina man a. Iarangsan ong aigija, 
aganenggipara badia jolni mande, Damra jolnima ba 
Baghmarachipaknisama ukoba uina man a. 
Apsan ku*sikko aganoba sakgni manderangni ganratanirang 
7 
apsan tiktak ong rongja. Dingtang dingtang a selrangni gimin 
indake onga. Agangimin gita biap a dokni kri, saming maidake 
janggi tanga uni kri aro chonmitingo skia manbagipa bewalrangni 
kri mandeni agananirang dingtang ongatako mana. Un baksana 
sakantini aganani dingtangmancha vewalrang dongengkua. 
SPEECH SOUNDS (AGANANIO GAM ANIRANG) 
Mandeni ku sik agananiko sandie niode, ku agangipa 
sakantian bang a dingtang dingtang gam anirangko ba ‘speech 
sound’-rangko jakkala ine nikgen. Indiba mongsonggipa name

ma ekatna man gipa ‘sound unit'-rangde (ganrani bakrang) arini 
ning'osa ong'aia. Ia ‘sound unit’- rangko Englisho Phonem es 
minga. “Pap" [p h ap] kattao skanggipa ‘p’-ni gam a gnigipa ‘p’-ni 
gamaoni dingtanga. Skanggipa ‘p‘-na kirsikoniko balwa ta'rake 
wata aro ‘ph’ gita ganrata. Gnigipa ‘p’-nade balwa wata dongja. 
Indake ‘p’-ko dingtang dake gamatoba; sound unit-de Ip/ 
gesasan. 
/k/ aro /t/ sound unitrangba indaken apsan onga. Achikkuo 
‘p’-ko /p/ba/ph/ gita gam atoba ortode dingtang ong rongja. Jekai 
Achik asolrang “kam” -ko “kham” ine ganrata. Gipin jatni 
bewalko rachapgiparang balwa wataniko bilakatgija [kam] ine 
gamatronga. Jedake gam atoba ortode dingtang ong ja. Indiba 
Hindiode katta mingsao /p/ ni pal /ph/ ko on jotode orto dingtang 
ong sranggen. Jekai [pal] ni orto dikdiksa ba mikiapsa aro [phal] 
ni orto ‘bite’. Apsan dake, an chingoni mitam ‘sam’-ko ‘sham’ ine 
ganrata. Ganrasa dingtangaia aro knatoanisa dongja; indiba 
ortode apsanaia. English-o ‘same’-ko ‘shame’ ine aro ‘sit’-ko ‘shit’ 
ine gam atode orto dingtang ong an baksanaba kratcha aniko 
ra baa. Jekai - 
(i) Good Night! Shame to you. (Same to you] 
(iij Shit here. (Sit here) 
Onggija gam-atanichi, skanggipa sentencera kratchachinasa 
aro gnigipara a-pal re chinasa agana ga akjok. 
8 
SYLLABLE: 
Agananiko ba kattako tong tong dake bak daka. Bakprakko 
ba unit geprakko ‘syllable' minga. Kattako aganon balwako 
jakkala. Kuaganmitingo ka sopni balwako ra-gala. Indake 
kuaganmitingo kasoponiko balwako jakkalanikon ‘Pulmonic Air- 
Stream Mechanism’ ine agana. Balwako joljolde ragalja, dongdik 
dongdikesa ragala aro dondikmitingo balwako ra-napskaa. 
Balwako second-prako changbonga mang ra gala aro indake 
changprak balwa ra galania syllable ba katta tong ge*prakko 
gamataniming apsanea. Basakoba bilbate aro basakoba bil komie 
balwako ra-gala. Jekai -

pari kattao skanggipa syllableo balwa raken wata, 
baji kattao skanggipa syllableo balwa komikale wata. 
English-o indake banoba banoba rakbate gam atakon ‘Stress’ 
ine minga. ‘Pari’ kattao syllable gegni donga. Skanggipa ba gnigipa 
syllableo ‘stress’ ko done gam atoba orto aro kattani ma a 
dingtang ong ja. Indiba English-o ‘stress’ gamchatbea. ‘Present’ 
kattako skanggipa syllableo ‘stress’ done gam atode uan Noun 
ong-gen; indiba gnigipaosa stress-ko donode, uan Verb 
ong skagnok. Kattako gam atanioba vowelni gamani dingtang 
ong*skaa. 
SPEECH SOUND: 
Syllable ge prakko speech-sound rokom gni dake sualna 
mana. Uaranga - Vowelrang aro Consonantrang. 
English-o vowel ge-gniko gam-dimatgipako diphthong ine 
minga. Indiba A-chikkuo diphthong dongoba anxhing uko vowel 
ge*gni gitan ra aia, ra dimrongja. Jekai, ‘maekae’ (maikai) kattao 
‘a’ aro ‘e’ ni ganrako namen ma ekatna man a. Indiba - ai - ko 
diphthong channa man a. 
A-chikkuo gam-anirangko da alonaba name sandie sea 
dongkuja. Haida sandiani donggenchimode badia kattarango ro*e 
gam atgipa vowelko jakkala aro badia kattarango kan-dike 
gam atgipa vowelrangko jakkala uko ma siani donggenchim.

A CHIKKUO KATTA RIKANI ARO BARIANI 
Axhikrang antangtangko “Mande” ba abri achurango donga 
ma*ate ‘Axhik” ina. Kutangko “Mande Kusik” ba “A'chik Ku-sik” 
ine uamang agana. ‘A-chik Ku sik” katta minggniko ritchudime, 
indiba kan-dikate, anxhing “Axhikku” ine agana. 
Axhik Ku sik Tibeto- Burman Kuambini Bodo tokoni onga. 
Axhikkuko mongsongbate Meghalayani Garo Hillsni District 
ge-gittamo, Khasi Hillsni West Khasi Hills aro Ri Bhoi 
Districtrangni mitam bakrango, Assamni Kamrup, Goalpara aro 
Karbi Anglong a-dokrango, Tripurao, West Bengalni Cooch Behar 
aro Jalpaiguri jolrango aro Bangladeshni Mymensingh aro Dacca 
jolrango agana. Axhikku Tibeto- Burman Ku ambini gipin 
Ku'sikrangming, jekai Kachan aro Koch, apsananga. 
Kamrup jolo agangipa Axhikku Garo Hillso agangipa 
Axhikkuoni on-tisa dingtanga; mongsongbate kattarango. 
Dingtangani agrejani gimin uko A chikkuni bipek mingsa 
insrangnade man ja. Garo Hillsni a palo agangipa Axhikkuo, 
Assamese aro Bengali Ku-sikrangni bilmananichi, ra srogipa 
kattarang bang bea. 
Itihasni gita, Axhikrang Ambi Asong Tibetoni reongkaton 
ramadilo dol dingtang dingtang ong aha. Dolrang repriprae, 
dolantian a-dok gipino songdong-axhaoba Ku-ambikode 
rim kingkingkuaha. Indiba doltangrangoni ma ekatako man e aro 
samtangtango jakkalenggipa Ku sikrangni bilmananichi, ia 
dolrangni agangipa Axhikku Ma ambi Axhikkuoni katta 
jakkalao, katta gam-atao ka sne dingtangangaha. Indake 
Axhikkuoni Bipek Ku-sikrang nakatbaaha. A-chikkuni Bipek 
Kusikranga Abeng, Atong, Awe, Chibok, Chisak, Dual, Megam, 
Matchi, Matjangchi, Gara Gan-ching aro Ruga. Ia Bipek Ku-sikrang 
mingsa minggipinoni agre dingtanggrikja; indiba bipekantion

dingtanganirang dongaia aro ia dingtanganiko Atong Ku*siko 
niksengbata. Garo Hillsni a dok gipinrangni manderangming 
agangrikna neng-beani ong-jaoba, Atongkode ma-sina 
nengniktoka. 
14 
A we bipekkon da o A'chikku ine, pilak sea jotani, skiani 
aro kamagipa gadango sason ka anina jakkalenga. Garo Hillsni 
bijangchijolo donggiparang salsalanti rea-doao ia Kusikkon 
agana aro iakon rachakgimin Kusik ine ra-gataha. A beng ba 
Am beng Kusikko Garo Hillsni saliramjolo agana. A*we 
Ku sikkora Garo Hillsni salgro aro salgro-salaramni abrirango 
aro ia a*brirangni japarango donggipa a kawerango agana. Atong 
Ku sikko Garo Hillsni South Garo Hills Districto Simsang ka-ma 
a kawejolo, salgipeng-salaram a brirango agana. Chibok aro Ruga 
Ku-sikrangko Garo Hillsni salgipengjolo bang-gija songrangosan, 
mongsongbate Dareng chibisikjolo aro Dalu sepangjolo Bugi 
chisam songrango agana. East Garo Hillsni Williamnagar jolo 
agangipa Ku-sikara Matchi. Jatchi a bidingni salgipengjolo aro 
Darengming Simsang gisepjolo agangipa Ku-sikara Gara 
Gan-ching. Matabeng ba Matjangchi Ku-sikkora Matchiko 
agangipa salgro-salaram aro Am-beng ku-ko agangipa saliram 
abrijolni gisepo donggipa adoko agana. Me-gamko Garo Hills 
Khasi Hills simajolo agana. Cooch Behar, Jalpaiguri aro 
Mymensingho agangipa Achikku Bengali Kusikming 
brinchapbeahani gimin uko ku gipin gita dakniktokpilaijok. 
A CHIKKU KATTARANGKO CHIMONGDIMANI 
Achikkuo kattarangna dilja, indiba uarangko 
tom chimongani chusokjani gimin bang-a changon katta tiktak 
nangnikako manningja. Garo Hillsona Baptist Missionaryrang 
re-baosa A-chikrang A chikku kattarangko tom-chimongna gisik 
nangna a-bachengaha. A-chikkuni me-suani aro katta bang-aniko, 
Katta Agana, Katta Saling, Dania, Ajea, Doroa, Manggrapa aro 
uandakgipa atchu-ambini chasongoni sea-jota gri 
aganrikrikbaanirang aro ripingbaenggiparangan sakki ona. Mite 
amuao, matburingrangni an-chiko on-e minganirango, bobil

dingna re-o aro bobil ame sko sote re-bapilmitingo minga 
de-anirango katta jelaniko nika. Agan-Me-apa, Katta-Jikse aro 
donmitape agananirangba A chikku kattanade kangalja ine 
paraka. Aganrikrikbaanirango basakobade mitam kattarangko 
jakwata aro mitamara jakkaljani gimin marame ja manode 
gimasranga. 
1788-69 bilsirango Daccani (Dhaka) Commissioner John 
Elliott A-chik kattarangko chimongdimna jotton ka-chengaha.

1800 bilsio Francis Hamilton, English, Bengali, Koch, Rabha, Garo,

tosusae niaha. A chik kattarangko tom chimongna gittamgipa 
jotton ka aniko Assamona re bachenggipa American Baptist 
Missionary Reverend Nathan Brown dakaha. William Robinson

ACHIKKUO KATTA RIKANI 
Achikku kattarangko tom chimongani bidingo niani 
ja mano, A*chikkuo katta rikani bewalrang aro cholrangni gimin 
niskana. Aganbagimin gita, A chikku kattarang bang sranga; 
indiba ua katta banaidape, katta ra sroe apalrikrikkuenga. 
Kusikde tanggipa, wa sagipa, tom tom dongaigijagipa bostusa. 
Ku-gipinrangoniko pilak Ku sikrangan ra*sroa aro nanga katta 
dongjaode tarigitala. Gital gital chanchianirang nakaton aro bostu 
gitalrang nakatdapdapon, dongenggipa kattarangchi uarangko 
talatna man ja. Nikmanchana man gipa bosturangna katta 
bikotna altukala; gisikosan dongna man aigiparangna ba 
chanchianirangna katta bikotania nengmanchagipa kam ong a.

Jajajiji dakjokode, adita kattarangko ra e ro e talatanikosa 
dakrongaia. 
KATTA RIKANI BEWALRANG /CHOLRANG 
1. KU SNINGANI (Imitation/ Onomatopoeia) 
Maiba bostuko uni gam ani kri biming on*aniko iano 
miksonga. Kattani gam anian uni bidingo chanchianiko 
mesokskaa. Nambatgipa mesokanide do po kattaan ong aia. 
Do po kattani skanggipa katta tong do - “ do'o ” kattani 
skanggipa katta tong ong a. pQ katta tongko ua do oni “po po” 
ine mikoaoniko ra a. Tari-tilonge pantena balgipakode pahap ine 
A-chikrang agana. Ian balgipa mechikni ichi uchi re*jojoe, aganjol 
ka dingjol dake, gana china pil ing pil ap dakaoni ba ba ra 
gam aoni a bachengana. 
Ia katta rikani bewalko jakkale Thrani sambajolo donggipa 
17 
a gani manderangni katta rikna changani aiao inmanani onga. 
Bostu brearango ramram plasticko jakkalbea. Plastico maikoba 
sikaton ba uoniko maikoba ra-ongkaton gam 'a. Uko rinrchepoba 
jekjek jakjak gama; unasa uamang plastico maiba bostuko 
sikaniko nangnikode, " iekiako” chipchina agana. Uamang “bike” 
•naba katta manjae, katta gital bikota. Bike reangode toptop 
taptap gam a; indake gam-anan uko toptop gari intoka. 
Jingjingbeng aro jetrangni bimingba uarangni mikoani gamaniko 
jarike ongipa bimingrangsa. 
2. SOKCHUANI (Suffixing) 
Japang kattani bon-ao sokchueba katta gitalko rika. Ja-pang 
katta nitoa-ko rana. Ia kattana-ni sokchuaniko on ode, katta 
gital “nitoani” ong kata.- gipa sokchuaniko on-ode, gipin katta 
“nitogipa”-ko mana. 
Kosako on gipa ja pang katta nitoa Verb ong a; “nitoani ” 
Abstract Noun aro “nitogipa”-ra Adjective. Indake sokchuani 
cholko ra-e gital kattarangko, part of speech dingtang ong-gipa 
kattarangko man -a. Indiba iano mingsako nika. "Nitoa” kattako 
“Vowel elision” dakgija ba bon-kamgipa katta tong -a ko ragalgija 
-ni ko joljol sokchuaia. Indiba “ nitoeipa” kattanade ja pang

kattani bon-kamgipa katta tongko, jean iano -a vowel, 
ra galchengesa - gipa katta tongko sokchue katta gital rika. 
Uni gimin an ching indake aganna man a : Sokchuani cholko 
ra-e katta gital rikon, ja pang kattani bon-kamgipa katta tongko 
basakoba ra gala aro basakobade ra galja. mi sokchuaniko 
jakkalon, ma sisretani dongjana gita, mesokani mingsako ra-na. 
“Anga” Pronounko ja pang katta gita ra-na. ‘Anga” Nominative 
Caseo ong a. Uko Genitive Caseo donode “Angni” ong a. Iano 
Anga aro Angni katta minggnini Part of Speech dingtang ong-ja; 
minggnian Pronoun ong-e dongaia. “Angni” kattao -ni katta tongni 
kamara Genitive Case marker ba Genitive Caseko mesokgipasa. 
3. ON JOTANI (Infixing) 
On-jotaniko jakkalgipa bang-gija Kusikrangoni Achikkuba 
mingsa ong-a. Englisho sokchuani aro on-dapchengani (Suffixing 
ba Prefixing) donga; on-jotanide dongja. A-chikkuora sokchuani 
aro on-jotani donga; on-dapchengani dongoba chong-diksasan. 
18 
A chikkuo onjotaniko dakon, Part of Speech pangnande 
dingtangrongja; ortosa dingtang onga. Cha-a kattako japang 
katta gita ra na. Cha preta kattani miksongania nangana bate, 
dugapile cha-a; cha-kusia . name tik ong e cha-ja; cha-simaia . 
cha kamaia. jringnan chaa; cha-asia . akal karap ba cha-ani 
dongja. 
Katta tong onjota baksana sokchuaniko on-dape katta gital 
riknaba mana. Jekai : cha chakani . tala batirang; cha-chakram . 
mi cha-ronggipa biap / kutturi. 
4. BAKKANDIKATANI (Abridgment) 
Mitam kattarangko bakkandikate, katta gital gita sea aro 
Dictionaryoba uarangko maate mesoka. Ia bakkandikatgimin 
kattarangni ortode dingtangja. Jekai: balwani pal bal: jajongni 
pal ja. Ia bakkandikatgimin kattarangko mongsongbate poedo 
seanio jakkalbata. 
5. CHUKOPGRIKANI (Telescoping) 
Katta minggniko chukopgrike ba sikpokgrikeba Achikrang 
katta rika. Ia bewalo katta rikanio, mitamnade badiaba

vowelrangko aro katta tongrangko gala; mitamnade galja. 
(a) Vowel ba katta tong ra-galgijagipa 
Matburing katta chu kopgrike rikgimin katta mingsa ong a. 
Mat -ara jonturangna jakkalgipa katta. Buring inon bol-waa 
dikgipa biapko miksonga. Indakgipa biapo donggipako an-ching 
“matburing” ine agana. Iano dingtang dingtang katta minggniko 
ra-dime katta mingsako ongata.Katta minggniko ra-dimani chinan 
dongjajok. “Buringni mat” inode, buring katta skang 
gaakgenchim; indiba uko ja mansa dona. Minggipinara, katta 
minggnionikon vowel ba katta tong ra-galani dongja. Sambijak 
kattaba apsan bewalo rikgimin ong a. “Mai samko song a?” inon 
miming cha-na song gipa bijakni gimin sing enga. A chikrangni 
ja pang me-surangbijakarigipa ong-a. Jekai : gal'da, garu (me-jak), 
arobak, ta bolchu bijak aro uagita. Uni gimin sam aro song-na 
jakkalgipa bijak ia katta minggniko ra-dime A-chikrang me-suna 
sambijak kattako jakkala. Sam aro bijak kattarangko joljol ra-dime 
19 
katta rikaia; vowel ra galani ba katta tong den*sotani dongja. 
(b) Vowel ba katta tong ra galgipa 
Katta minggniko chu kopgrike katta gital rikon, katta 
gitalrango badiaba vowel ba katta tongko aksotgala. Te rik kattako 
mesokani gita rachengna. Kattani skanggipa katta tong -l£“bite” 
kattani gnigipa katta tong on g*a. -nk katta tongara “rika” kattani 
skanggipa katta tong ong a. Uni gimin gadang gadang rikgipa 
bitena “teTik” bimingko on*a. Ia katta gitalko rikon gitcham katta 
winggnionikon katta tong ge prak aksotgala. Bite kattaoniko bi- 
katta tongko aro rik a kattaoniko -a katta tongko ra’gala . 
Mi nggipin gisik nangataniara,bite aro rika katta minggnion “raka” 
(-) dongja; indiba gital katta “teTik” - ode raka (*] nakata. 
Minggipin katta “chining”-ko niskana. “chining” kattako, 
minggni kattarangoniko ong ata : chi aro ning*a; chongmotan, 
apalo nikao jokgipa chi ong ja, ning joljol jokgipa chisa. “Chining” 
kattako ongaton, skanggipa katta chi-ko maming dingtangatgija 
donchenga. Uni ja mano, ning a katttani skanggipa katta tong 
ning -ko chi-na sokchudapa; indiba “raka” (•) aro - a katta

tongkode donangaijok. Chining inon a palo ong-ja, chini ning-osa 
ineba miksonga. 
Kosako on giminrangko nie, iakoba an ching aganna man a 
: Chu kopgrikanichi katta rikon, katta gitchamrango “raka” (•) 
dongoba, uko katta gitalo ra ja; “raka” ( ) dongjaoba, uko katta 
gitalo ra*gata. 
Chukopgrikachi rikgipa Achikku kattarang 
bang-a.Uarangoni mitamrangara : me dik (mi + dikte), sam*dik 
(sam + dikte), chibawil(chi + bawila), an gil (an +bigil), 
nokmong (nok + mongsonggipa / mongsonga). 
6. DONBRINANI (Compounding) 
Bimang aro bewal apsananggipa bosturangni bimingrangko 
donbrinachi aro maioba orto apsananggipa verbrangko 
donbrinani cholchiba katta gital rikna man a. Katta gitalni ortoo, 
ja-pang kattarangni orto baksa nangrimani donga aro bang*bata 
changon uarang apsan part of speech-ni ba kattani ma-ani ong a. 
“Re a- doa” kattako mesokani gita ra na. Ia kattara donbringipa 
katta ba compound word ong a. “Re a” inon rama re*ako aro 
20 
“doa” inon abri ba chukala biapko maldoako miksonga. Ichi 
uchi reon rama apsanakoba re a, a ding dongode donaba gaaka. 
Rea aro doakattarang orto apsanangoba dingtanganiba dongtaia. 
Uarangko ra*dime rea-doa inon re ruraani ba songreaniko 
miksonga; indiba ja pang kattarangni ortooni katta gitalni orto 
chelja. 
Donbrino iarangko nika: (i) Katta gitalni orto ja pang 
kattarangni ortoko man chapa. (ii) Katta gitalko rikon, ja pang 
kattarangko bimang dingtangatja. (iii) Ja pang kattarangko 
sedimja; jatchigriko kan dikbegipa salsretaniko on*a. 
Donbrinachi rikgipa mitam kattarangara : sam-bol, bol-wa a, 
doo-mat, chaani-ringani, tuani-dongani, nika-nia, songa-rita. 
7. DONMITAPANI (Euphemism) 
Kragija ku-attogija, aganronggija, jakkegija kattarangko aro 
duk saknaaniko ra bagipa kattarangko Achikrang tongtong 
aganja. Donmitape aganesa ke gijako ke ata, dukni kattako

dim demata, ku*attogijako knatoata aro ka ananganiko ra rikata. 
Sia katta mandena dukni ong a aro sepangbatgiparang aro 
ka sabegiparangni sia bon ani kattako rang san knaode 
ka achakna amjae basakobade knagipa an tangba sipaa. Uni 
gimin, gipin mande siomangba, amikkade re angsrangiokna, 
katangsrangjokna. dongjaiokna ine donmitape agane, rakgipa 
kattako on-tisa nom ata. Ia apsankon ianggi gala ineba agana. 
Mitama be a wila inskaa. 
Minggipin knatosranggijagipa kattara ki a. Bi*sarangna 
jakkalode knatokala; dal giminrangnade namen knatojabea. Ia 
kattara rang*sanan mitchiani, nitogija bostuni bimangko mandeni 
gisikona ra-baa.A chikrang unasa sel tagija kattarangko donmitape 
agana. Kra kattani palo A chikrang a pal re a, rorama, re-branga, 
buring napa aro uarang gita donmitape agana. Kattarangko niode, 
nokningo ba nokni a palo, a pal re chakanirangko tarikujamiting 
somoeko gisiko nikata. Da omangba song diltuarangchi 
re angode, a brangna . buring napna nangaiengpitti. 
Donmitape agangipa mitam kattarangara : mera pasadapa, 
ja ma ja-si nanga, be’entang, chelo a bri totgni, jikma jikse daka, 
chame man a, silpa-nampagija, kombol bia, anrpak ko san turima 
aro uagita. ~ ~ 
21 
8. KATTANI MAAKO JUJAGRIKATANI 
(Changing the Part of Speech) 
Kattarangni maako jujagrikateba, mingsa kattakon 
apsananggipa ortoko on e katta gital gita jakkala. Mesokani gita 
“chi a” kattako rana. Achikrang, mongsongbate skangkalgipa 
salrango aro songadamrango chao chini bang-e one ringa, maina 
mitamde chimanchaakosa namnika; chigijakode cha ringa gita 
daknikja ina. la chie ringaoni abachenge mitamrangde “ chia 
ringgenma?” ine roanggiparangko sing a. kattade Verbsa; 
indiba uko Nounni bimango done, cha kattani palo jakkala. 
Indaken, " Meseng cha-osa be en an senga”, "Ka a chaosko ding a 
komikaia” ingipa sentencerangko jakkalako knaa. Ka-a katta Verb 
ong-a; ia sentenceode uko Noun gita jakkala. Kattani ma-a

dingtangahaon, ua kattako katta gital gita ra e, Dictionaryoba 
ma eke mesokronga. 
Kattani ma*ako jujagrikate jakkalgitalgipa kattarang 
bang-beade ongja. 
9. KATTA MINGGNI MINGGITTAM JAKKALDIMANI 
(Using two or three words together) 
Orto dingtanganggipa katta minggniko ba una bate 
jakkaldime, orto dingtanggipa kattako jakkala. Jekai : ianggi gala. 
langgi ar o gala kattarang orto dingtanga aro uarangni katta ma-aba 
dingtanga. Indiba uarangko dondimon, uni ortora sia 
ongskaa.Iandakgipa gipin kattarangara : iako ra-a. kamko ka na 
an tango ra a; iako man a. demikkang nika ba bi sa atchia; palang 
ola. arate tue donga; kni boksrua. budepaa; mikka bit chi, mikka 
birong/ tottak; bolma be dapa. a sel ong-a. Katta minggniko 
jakkaldimoba, minggnini gisepo dashko ba kan-dikbegipa 
saltonganiko jakkalja. 
10. BIMANG BA GUNKO NIE KATTA RIKGITALANI 
(Coinage) 
Chasong gitalni chanchianirang aro bikotgital bosturangna 
Achikkuo katta gri aro je donggipaba tik ong-e talatna chuongja. 
A-chikkuosan ong-aija, gipin skia man-gipa silrorogimin 
a songrangba ia apsan neng-nikaniko chagronga. Science aro 
22 
Technologyni bidingo katta man jabata. Indaken Achikkuchi 
seaniko dakomangba Science, Technology, Television, Video, 
Computer, Music System, Oxygen aro uandaka kattarangko 
ku’gipinon jakkalkuna ga akaia. Indiba Axhikrangni gisik 
taningba kam kaanio, maikoba rikgitalanio jamanchakja. Banga 
ku gipin kattarangnan katta rikgitalaha. Cinemako iawilwat ina. 
Ia kattako lagring aro wilwat kattarangoniko ra-a. “Wilwat” 
kattani ortora ichi uchi mojingjengani ba ilengani. Cinemao 
kal'giparangni jagringrang ichi uchi re-jojoe, wilwil dawil mesae 
mesokani kride, uko jawilwat mingna krabea. Photo cameranade 
chipiaerine ba iachipchang kattako jakkala. Katta gital 
minggnikon ia gring aro chipa /chipchanga kattarangko ra-dime

ongata. Photo ka-on jagringko chipe ba chipchange raa. 
Televisionna bairong kattako rika. Scientistko gisik aning bitgipa 
ine pe'a. Katta mingsanarichi dontojae, bangbata kattarangko 
ra dime talatani gitasa ortoko ona. Gisik. aa. ning a aro bita 
katta japang mingbriko rae, Scientistna biming on a. 
Longitudeko a-gilmrong ine agana. A a. bigil aro mrong 
kattarangko ra-dime agilmrong kattako ong-ata. A a kattaoniko 
gnigipa katta tongko aro bigil kattani skanggipa katta tongko 
katta gitalo gala. Mrongni ortode nokni mongsonggipa krong. 
Latitudeko amatchang ine agana. A-matchang kattao ja pang 
katta minggni donga : a a aro matchanga: skanggipa aro gnigipa 
ja pang kattarangoni bonkamgipa katta tongrangko katta gitalo 
ra ja. Matchangani ortode denjangjanga ba duule den a. 
Equatorko a sengki minga. A a aro seng-ki kattako dondime 
a sengki kattako rika. Equator a-gilsakni seng ki ong a. Oasisko 
a sosi ba a chiiim minga. A a sosigipa ba a-a chijimgipa ine iano 
miksonga. Ia katta rikgitalani bewalo bostuni bimang, bewal ba 
gunko nie biming on a. 
Katta rikgitalanina A chikrangoni rasong man na 
krabatgipade Harendra W. Marak Vernapaan ong a. Pa Marak 
katta man-manchaa. Gisiktaning wa-saani gimin, taning rokroke 
katta rikgitalna ua changbea. Wal-imkol (volcano), a-bri nokma 
(mountain), chiontim (waterfall), suuri ranggap (glacier) aro uagita 
bang-bea uni bikotgimin kattarang donga. 
11. KAMKO NIE KATTA RIKGITALANI 
Bostuni kamko nieba A chikrang katta rika. Bijina silrengsi 
23 
kattako jakkalanian namgipa mesokani onga. Biji kattade 
kugipinoniko ra'srogipasa; silrengsi kattade A chikkuan. 
Skangonin Achikrang rengsiko aro rengsi jotna wa-dingko 
jakkala. Silko manon, silchi rengsi sikna aro kilding sikna tarie 
jakkalbaa. Aroba sepango donggipa gipin jatrangni biji jakkalako 
nike jakkalpaenggen. Rengsi sika gita bijichi koani gimin, una 
silrengsi kattako on-a ine rimchipna man-a. 
Minggipin katta silkam ko nina. Gojal dato ba maikoba

dokdalgepna nangode kamchakaniko nanga. Bolo kanvchakna 
man's, indiba bolara be priaia aro baija; siloniko tarigipa 
kam chakani beningja aro baia. Indake kanrchakgipa silko ba 
silkamko jakkala. futaginako tarigiparang ba silkamko jakkalbea 
Wal-tot kattaba kamko nike on gimin aro waal aro tot a 
katfarangoniko ragipa biming ong-a. Waako / bu duko bn 
roongko totgr ike natdudete wa al ongkatataonisa wal tot 
bimingko man a. Iandake kamko nie bikotgipa katta gitalrangoni 
mingsara rengreng sil. Bicycleko ba cycleko niode rengrengsil 
mingna krabea. Silni chakka rengrenganganiko ba chakka gegni 
wilwile re anganiko nie dongrikgipasa ia kattako bikotna nanga. 
A garang ia kattakode jakkalchenga ina; indiba sawamancha 
sing-ode darangba talja. 
12. KU GIPINRANGONIKO RA SROANI 
(Direct Borrowing) 
1806 jolrangoni, chong-motan British sasonni abachengaoni 
aro Baptist Missionaryrangni Garo Hillso kam a bachengaoni, 
English kattarang A-chikkuo bilongen ja napaha. Chukala 
gadango poraigipa A chikrang bang batrorobaon, salantini 
ramram agangrikanirangoba English kattarangko jakkalchapaha. 
Bengali aro Assameserangming sambagrik donge aro ja mano 
dongbrine, anti-a kang cha-rime, ia ku sik minggni gitan Persian 
aro Indo-Aryan kattarangkoba A'chikkuo ra-napaha. A chikku 
kattarang dongomangba, ra srogimin kattarangko jakkalnan 
jakkenikbattoka.

Kattarangko baibaira ■ sroaigija , A chikkuo gam 'ani bewalko 
jarike, banoba vowelni aro banobara consonantni gam-aniko 
dingtangate kattarangko jakkala. “r” ni pal “1” consonantko 
ragatskaaniko “Isol” aro “bichal” kattarango nika. “Dukh” aro 
“sukh” kattarangni bonkamgipa consonantrangko balwa wate 
gamata. Achikkuo “k” ko katta tongni bon-kamao gamaton 
balwa watgija gamatani gimin duk, suk inesan gamataia. “Maida” 
kattani skanggipa vowelko dingtangate moida ine A chikkuo 
jakkala. Un baksana “f” ni pal “p”, “z” ni pal “j”, “v”, ni pal “b”, 
“sh” ni pal “s”-ko A chikkuo jakkalskaa, maina mingchenggipa 
gamanirang A-chikkuo dongja. Jeba ongbo, iandake ra gate

ku sikrangoniko bang kalen ra sroaha ine nika. English ku sik 
Indo-Aryan ku sikrangonikoba ra sroaha. Jekai : machhang, 
pucca, cummerbund, catamaran aro uagita.

raka tani chin; (•) ia chin; a sign showing abrupt cut between 
syllables”.

Apsan Bakni Tok 4, o “ raka” - ni gimin indine janapa: “The 
added sign (■) is used to indicate the abrupt cutting off of the 
sound at the close of the syllable after which this is placed; as 
naa , to rise out of and na-a, you; soa, to decay and soa, to cause 
to burn.”

“RAKA” GAM ANI MAIKAI ONG KATA 
Vocal cord ge gnini gisepo donggipa bangbanggipa biapko 
glottis minga. Vocal cordrangara mikkangchipakde joradima, 
indiba janggilchipakde ma ekgrika; unigimin uarangko 
nangdimatnaba aro ekatnaba man a. Ia vocal cordrangko 
nangdimatsrangode ba nangdimatna gitik dake donode, glottal 
sound ba raka-ni ganraniko ong katata. 
Achikkuode, glottisko tang onate ba basakoba chikakko 
tang doate, gingting gita balwa joke kataniko champengskae, 
raka -ni gam aniko ong katata. Ong katbaenggipa balwako 
chipchangrika aro uni ja mantapgipa gam aniko ba katta tongko 
(syllable) ganratskana gita, agananio jakkalgipa ku sikni bakrang 
maekanga. 
Ongkatbaenggipa balwako vocal cordrang 
dikdiksanamangmang chipchangrike, uko watpilon glottal sound 
ba raka-ni gam a ong kata. Raka -ni gam ani Arabic ku-siko aroba 
lekka-pora dongpagijagipa English manderangni agangipa 
ku sikrangoba gnang.

Ong ronggija “Raka” Jakkalanirangoni Mitamrang 
(a) “Raka” On dapani: A chikkuni gam ani bewalko aro 
mitam Achikku kattarangni ongbaaniko nion, japang kattarango 
“raka” donggijaniko nika. Mesokani gita, ja pang katta minggniko, 
mi aro dikte -ko sikpokgrike ba chu-kopgrike (telescope ka e) 
me dik kattako ong ata. Ja pang katta minggnion raka dongja, 
indiba uarangni ong atgipa katta me dik- ode raka -ni gam ani 
donga. Apsan dake gam ani dingtanganiko te rik kattaoba nika. 
Skanggipa katta tong - te bite kattaoni aro gnigipa katta tong -rik 
rika kattaoni ong a. Ia ja pang katta minggnion raka dongjaoba, 
uarangni ong-atgimin katta te rik -ode raka donga. 
(b) “Raka”-ko Ra-galani : Ja pang kattarango raka dongoba, 
sikpokgrike ong'atgimin mitam kattarangode, raka- ni gamaniko 
ra gala. Elision ba gam ani mingsako ra'galaniko demechik kattao 
nika. Demechik kattani ja pang kattarangara de aro me-chik. 
Gnigipa ja pang kattao raka -ni gam*ani dongoba, uko ra-gale 
demechik inesa seskaa ba aganskaa. 
Minggipin indakgipa kattara chianine . Ia kattako ja pang 
katta minggittamoniko ongata: Chi, a-a, aro ning-a. Bonkamgipa 
ja pang katta minggnion raka- ni gam ani donga: indiba uarangko 
32 
sikpokgrike chianing kattako ongaton, a a aro ning o 
kattarangoniko katta tong mingprakko ragalasan ong-aigija, raka - 
ni gamanikoba ragala. 
(c) Rasrogimin Kattako A-chikku Dakatani: 
Ki-tap katta Sanskrit "kitab”-oni Assamese aro Bengali 
ku sikrang gita A chikkuo jamapa. A’chikku kattarang 
“b” consonanto bon-egipa dongja. Indake “kitab” kattani 
bon kamgipa consonant “b” ko “p” dakatskaa. Ia kattana raka - 
ko on dape uko Achikku katta ki tap dakatsranga.

Mitamrang uko raka gri agana aro sea. Mitamrangara raka g nang

gipin kattarango gita ong gija ia kattao raka- ni gam ani onkala 
gadango ong a. 
Unigimin ia kattako uia ine segija, raka donge u-ia ine seon

(ii) Ruuta kattao, “u” vowelni ganraniara ro - a aro uko 
mesokna gita gnigipa “u”-ko on-a. Indiba word-stress ba kattako 
ga-jete aganani ruuta kattao ga-akon, an ching “u” vowelko 
kan-dike gam atskae, raka -ni ganraniko on dapskaa. Indake 
ong-on, gnigipa “u” vowelko, gnigipa katta tongni skanggipa oikor 
33 
dakatskaa. Indake ruuta kattako mai obostao jakkala, uko niesa 
raka totna man e, choljokate donon nambata. 
(iii) Katta mingsao raka changgni totna man-ama manja 
ineba jajaani nakatronga. Mesokani gita ka chongmota kattako 
ra-na. Ia kattani japang kattarangara ka a aro chone-mot. 
Achikku kattarangko ongaton, katta tong ba gamani gesako 
on-jotdapna ba ra-galna mana ine nikbamanaha. Iako chanchie, 
gnigipa raka -ko ragalanio maming onggijani dongja. Una agreba, 
A chikku kattarango skanggipa katta tongni jamantapsan raka 
dongrongbataia. 
Minggipinara, katta mingsao sambagrik katta tong ge-gnion

raka -ko onode, kattako joljol name gam atna man ja. Unigimin 
indakgipa kattarango raka- ni gamani gesakosan donaio nambata. 
BONATANI 
1. Raka totaniara kattani gamaniko dingtangatasan 
ongaigija, kattani mangsonganikoba dingtangata. Raka- ni kam 
indakpile mongsonggipa ongode, uko gale kattako seaniara 
A chikkuko gam-ani mingsao kangaldapatani ong a. Unigimin 
rakako jakkalaniko rongjrie ra na kraa. 
2. Seanirango raka-ko full-stop on a gitasa ka mao totronga. 
Raka- ni chong motgipa biap katta tong ge gnini gisepo, 
oikorrangna ontisa kosakkalaosa onga. Gipin chinko ba onggija 
biapko raka- na jakkalode kraja. 
3. Simsakgijani ba jajaani a - sel bang a ki tap/ lekka 
segiparangan raka totaniko gelsrangaia. laiong kattaoniko “ng”- 
ni gam aniko gale seode, jajo ong-aijok aro uan A chikku katta 
ong ja. Cha-a kattaoniko “h” -ko ra gale seode ca a ong aijok, 
jeon orto gri. Taio ba ca a ine seode ra-chaktogipa ong ja. Indake, 
raka- ni ganraniko galchange seode ra chaktoani ong ja aro banan 
gualani ong-a.

mingsani ning tuapiko aro gamchataniko ma sia. Raka -ko 
galchange seaniara A chikkuni gam-ani dondimgimin bewalko 
mikkim onatani ong a.

2. William Robinsonni “Garo Grammar” 
William Robinson bak dakkugijagipa Assamo Sorkarini 
Schoolrangni Inspector ongachim. Uni Garo Grammarni gimin 
seani March, 1849-ni Asiatic Journal Volume XVIII Part I-o nakata. 
Ia seani A’chikrangni bidingo bang gija talatchenganichi 
abachenga. 
Robinson indake janapa: ‘A chikrang seani chinrangko ba 
oikorrangko jakkalja; uamang una skang Bhotiarangni seani 
chinrangko ba oikorrangko ragatahaode, uamang ja mano ia 
manderangoni chel angsrangahani gimin aro a dok jitenggipa 
jatrangni chagronggimal neng-nikanirangni asel, ia oikorrangni 
chinmangba dongsrangjaha”. 
Noun katta bichongni ningo, affixrangko, chong motan 
kattarangna katta tong on dapgiparangko, jerangkon Robinson 
caserangni chinrang minga, Genitive caseko mesokgipa-ni, 
Accusative caseko mesokgipa -kho (-ko), Dative caseko 
mesokgipa -na, Instrumental caseko mesokgipa-chi, Ablative 
caseko mesokgipa nikho (-niko) aro Locative caseko mesokgipa 
katta tongrangko on a.

Mesokani minggniko on a:

talatgipa kattarang ba Adjectiverang uarangni talatenggipa 
Nounrangni mikkangoba ki sangoba re na man aia. Adjectiveni 
ki sango re*a ong-ode, ua Verbni kamkoba ka jola. “Mande nama” 
inon ua chu*sokgipa sentence ong aia. 
Adjectiveni bidingo seanio gipin gualanirangkoba nika. 
Ka*mao on-giparangko niode, gualaniko nikgen: 
The human hand Mandeni jak 
A wooden knife ' *Am bolni kethali

jakkalbata. “Am bolni kethali” ine jakkalakode knasrangja.

Good Nama

A house Nok

Janapaniba guala aro uni a selan Mesokanirangba ong tokja. 
A chikkuo maming saloba “nok” kattana bon kamgipa oikor “a”- 
ko sedaptaie uko Verb dakatja. Verbrangde “a” gam anio 
bontoka; mikkangchi dakgni / kagniko ba dakdaldal ka-daldalko 
janaposa dingtangaia. Mesokani: cha a, cha jok; tua, tujok; 
chagen, ringgen.

kattarango sesretanirang ba ma'sigija seanirang bang'bea.

bonchotaosa ong a, ine Robinson agana. Ia janapani tik onga;

donani bewalde segipani skaosa pangchakaia”. Ia janapaniko

re aniko sentenceo adita jujaata. 
3. E.G. Phillipsni “Outline Grammar of the Garo Language’ 1 
E.G Phillips Americaoni Baptist Missionary onge re bae 
a doko dangdike on mitingo A chik Ku sikko jakkalani 
niamrangko sandie niaha. “Outline Grammar of the Garo Lang- 
uage” -ko 1904 bilsio nakatata. Garo Literature Society uko 1988 
bilsio name chappa ka ata. Iano “Outline” ine on ani gimin, A chik 
Grammarni Bikrokkosan ianoniko ka dongchaksona man aia, 
che emanikode ong ja. 
Phillips ia seaniko dingtang dingtang A chik dolrangni aro 
jolantini agangipa ku sikrangni bidingo janape abachenga. 
Skanggipa Bakni skanggipa Toko ua Ortography ine Katta Bichong 
on*e, banan jakkalani gimin indake sea: “Sea-jotanina A chik Ku*- 
sikni ganranirangko chu onga gita tik ong e mesokna, ka mao 
on-gipa Roman oikorrangan chu*ongaia ine nika”. Ua ia

oikorrangko indake on a: 
a, b, c, d, e, g, h, i, j, k, 
1, m, n, o, p, r, s, t, u, w, 
aro on dapgimin chin (■) 
Brigipa Toko Phillips agana: “On dapgipa chinko ( ) katta 
tong matchoto ganraniko rang san aktonggalaniko ba 
dondikaniko mesokna jakkala”. Ia on-dapgipa chinko (*) 
A chikkuo raka ine agana aro badiaba kattako mingmitingo balwa 
wataniko gitoko rang san ra rikani gimin uko “glottal stop” minga. 
Glottisara vocal cord ge-gnini gisepo donggipa biap; vocal 
cordrangko nangdimaton, balwa naprurana man-ja.Mitam 
A chikku kattarangko aganon ia balwa ramako rang san chipe, 
balwako gam e watpila. Axhikku kattani ortoko on anio raka (') 
namen mongsonggipa ong-a. Uko tik ong'gipa biapo donanichi 
44 
kattani ortoko dingtangata. Jekai: 
soa Na-tok soa seenga. 
sera Na-tok so a simila. 
Ia katta tokgnini orto namen dingtanggrikbea. 
Ki-tapni Odhai 2,0 Phillips Nounrangko katta bichong ra-e, 
Gender (Me-a Mechik Ong-ani), Number (Bang-a Bang gijani) 
aro Case-rangni bidingo talataniko daka. 
Mandeni mea mexhik ong aniko kattachin mesoka ine 
agane, ua ia kattarangko Mesokanirang gita on a: ma a, paa, 
measa, me chik, pante aro metra. Bi-sanade, me a ba mexhik 
kattako on-dape me a bi sa ba mexhik bi - sa ine sea; 
matburingrangnara bima ba bipa kattako jaman seskaa ineba ua 
mesoka. 
A chikkuo bang ako mesokna Phillipsni on-gipa katta 
tongrangara -rang,-drang,-mang, ma'drang. Ua talatdapa: rang- 
an ramrambatgipa; drang-de darang-ko kan dikatanisakon. ‘Mang’ 
katta tongo ‘man chapatani’ donga ine agane, ua mesokaniko 
on a. Omedmang inode Omed aro uming dongpagiparangko 
miksonga, indiba Omedrang inode, Omed minggipa baditaba sak 
manderangko miksonga. Phillips ‘ma-dang’ katta tongko ‘mang’

katta tongming apsanata. 
Noun baksa chanani dongchapon, bang aniko mesokani 
dongja ineba ua talata. Jekai ‘mande sakbri’. Iano ‘mande’ kattana 
‘-rang’ ko on dapja. Noun baksa gipin rokomni Adjective ba 
talatgipa katta dongode, ‘-rang’ ko talatgipa kattanasa 
sokchudapa. Jekai: bol chugipa, bol chugiparang. 
Caserangko talaton, ua ‘matchu’ kattako Sangipa aro 
Bang-gipa chananio done, uarangko case mingantina mesoka. 
Odhai 3,o Adjectiverango Case mingantina on dapani, 
Adjectiverangko tosusaani aro Adjectiverangni Verbni kamko 
ka ani gimin talata. “Bite nama” inon, ‘nama’ kattara talatgipa 
katta ong'oba Verbni kamko ka*a ine ua mesoka. Cardinal 
numberrang, jekai sa,gni, gittam, bri aro ordinal numberrang, jekai 
sagipa, gnigipa, gittamgipa, brigipa, iarangkoba mesoka. 
45 
Numerical Prefixes ba Chananina Ondapgiparangko ua aditan 
janapa; uarangoni mitamrangara sak, tong, king, sik, dot. 
Pronounrangni rokomrangko Odhai 4,o janapa. Verbrangni 
bidingo Phillips Odhai 5,o aditan bakroe talataniko on*a. 
Verbrangni ma*arangara Neuter, Active aro Causal aro 
A chikkuode Passive Verbrangko jakkalbreja ine ua janapa. 
Rokomari Moodrango Sangipa aro Bang'gipa Chananirangko aro 
Tenseni rokomrangko jakkale Mesokanirangko ua aditan ona. 
Odhai 6,0 Adverbrangko aro Napsikgipa Adverbrangko 
(Inserted Particles) aro Odhai 7, ora Prepositionrangko talata. 
Phillipsni janapgipa Prepositionranga: baksa, gita, mu/mung, 
gimin, adam, a-sel, taltal, agre aro kingking. Ia ongiminrango 
asol Prepositionrang dongja aro ‘a dam’ inon maiko 
miksongachim talja. Da - ororode, A-chikkuo Preposition dongja, 
Postpositionsa donga ine jeraa. 
Odhai 8,0 Conjunctionni ningo on gipa kattaranga: indiba, 
indioba, ba, aro, indide, maikai; iarangoni ‘ba’ aro ‘aro’-ko 
Bengalioniko ra gata ine Phillips janapa. 
Odhai 9,o Interjectionni ning o on giparanga: hapbap, aiao, 
himangai, che, gose, rara, atchao, de, da nang aro nama. ‘Hai’

aro ‘mo’-koba ua Interjectiono napchapata. Atchikape A-chikkuko 
agangipade, ‘hai’ aro ‘mo’-ko aiao inmanani kattarang gita 
ra chakna man-ja. Phillips an tangba jajaen “Interjection mingna 
man-genkon” inesa dona. 
Ki tapni bon-kamgipa Odhaio iarangni bidingo 
Mesokanirangko on a: Ramram Sentencerang, Caserang, 
Pronounrang, Adjectiverang, Verbrang, Neuter Verbrang, 
Adverbrang, Adverbial Particlerang aro rokomari Sentencerang. 
Namnamgipa Mesokanirangko on a, indiba gisep gisep 
jajaanirangko nika. Jekai: Ramram Sentenceni ning o ia 
Mesokaniko on a: 
Askiko anga nienga. 
Ia sentenceo object ‘aski’-ko sentenceni a’bachengao on-a. 
Badiaba kattako niksengatna skosa, ia katta donani bewalko 
ja'rika. Iano ‘aski’ kattako sechenge niksengatani gimin, ia 
sentenceo aganchipilani donga. Ia aganchipilaniko parake seon, 
46 
sentence indake ongskagen: 
Askikosa anga nienga, gipinkode ong-ja. 
Phillips ia bako ‘nika’ aro ‘nia’ kattarangni ortoko ma-ate 
ona. Achikrangni jakkalronggija sentencerangko ua Caserangna 
Mesokanirangko on-mitingo jakkala. Uaranga: 
(i) Nang ni adatang ban songcbi re- a? 
(ii) Angni abitangni seni bimung Riksan. 
Skanggipa sentenceo, ‘ban’ kattani palo ‘badia’ aro ‘re-a’ 
kattani palo ‘reanga’ kattarang krabata. Gnigipa sentenceko, 
“Gumeko Riksan minga” inon seng-sote sena man-aigenchim. 
English Grammarchi ua bilmanatako man -aha ine iaba sakki 
mingsa. 
Pronounrang aro Adjectiverangna Mesokanirangko ongipao, 
Phillips Verb ‘joka’ aro Causative Verb ‘jokata’ -ko jakkala. Ua 
‘namnikbata’ kattako iandake pe rarone mesoka: 
namnikbata= adjective nama +verb nika+ verb bata 
Ia pe-raronanio, katta minggittamko ra e ‘namnikbata’ 
kattako ong-ata ine miksongakon. Indiba, ‘nama’ kattaba

Adjectiveni kamko ka-jolgipa Verbsa onga; ‘namgipa’ inosa 
srangsranggipa Adjective ong a. Verbrangna Mesokanirangoba 
adita jajaanirang nakata. Jekai: 
(i) Mande bisarang skulona re batokenga. 
An-ching ‘mande bi sarang’ ine aganrongja; ‘bi sarang’ inon 
chu-ongaia. 
(ii) Na-a angko dakchakjagenchim, anga nokko rikna 
manjawachim. 
Sentenceara Subjunctive Moodo ongani gimin 
‘dakchakjagenchim’ verbna katta tong ‘-ode’ ko on-dapna ga-aka. 
(iii) Uni re-chakata ruutaha, uni gimin ua nokchi 
sokangenggen. 
Indake agananio A chikrang verbna ‘-gen’ katta tongko 
sokchugija, ‘-gnok’ katta tongkosa sokchua. 
47 
(iv) Kam ka-gniko namedake kabo. 
“Kam ka-gniko” ineba Achikrang agana; indiba ianode gisik 
nangatna kupattianiko ba geetaniko onengani gimin, “Kagni 
kamko” inon sentence srangbata aro bilakbata. 
(v) Gong-rake bi sarangko okambo, sal re-angjok. 
‘Gong-rake’ kattani palo gipin Adverbko jakkalna 
man genchim. Ia sentenceo walnasiako miksongani gimin, “sal 
napangengjok” inon krabata, maina “sal re-angjok” inani ortode 
dingtang. 
Neuter Verbrang ine agangipa kattaranga: onga, donga, 
gnang, man-a, minga. 
Ia ki tapo segiminko niode, ia Grammar Kitap Bikroksan 
ong aija, bangen cheemani donga ine nika. Ku-tangni 
Grammarkon sena altuaja; indiba Phillips sandirikkote, 
sing sandie aro an tang ning-tue chanchiesa ia Ki-tapko sea ine 
nikna nengja. Ku-gipinchi seode, Kutangni bilmanani dongtelaia. 
Un baksana, una agane on timgipaba Ku-tangni Grammarni 
niamrangko kraa gitan uigipa aro agananio ba seanio jakkalgipa 
kattarangni / sentencerangni dingtanggrikaniko uigipa ong na 
nanga. Ki tap seenggipa English mande ong ode, agantime

ongipaba English Kusikko aditan uipana gaaka. 
Neng nikanirangko bang en chagronggenchimoba, Phillips 
namen jotton ka bee, Garo Grammarni niamrangko 
donchimongna gisik nangaha aro Garo Grammarna ga-chataniko 
A-chikrangna onangaha. 
4. Rev. E.G. Phillipsni “A-chik Grammar” 
‘A-chik Grammar” Ki-tapko 1981 bilsio Tura Book Room 
chappa ka-ataha. Ki tapko tok donani bimang tik ong a, man-gopa 
aro bakrangko cheembate ona. Phillipsba A-chikku Grammarko 
seanio, A-chikku Grammarko Ku gipin Grammarni niamrango 
pangchakatani bewaloni jokjajok. 
“Chanchisoani Kata” ine aganchenggipa bako Phillips iako 
janapa: “Mamung ku sikni grammarko skiode, ua ku-sikko tale 
aganani ba seani niamrangko uina man-a, aro ua niamrangko 
uiode, mandeskana aganon, ba katako seon, an tangtangni 
48 
chanchiako ba mangsongako talatna dakchakbea. Achik 
Grammarko skiode, uandaken dakchakani onggen. Pilak 
ku-sikrangko aganani/ seani niamrang apsan onga. A chik 
Grammarko skichengode, Kugipinni Grammarko ma sina 
dakchakbegen.” Phillipsni agana gitan Kusik jakkalani 
niamrangde apsanaia. Ku*sik mingsani grammarko uianiba 
Ku gipinni grammarko ma sianio dakchakbea. 
Phillips, Kattani aro Sentenceni definition ba talataniko 
ona. Ia talataniko poraigiparang ma siama ma sija nina gita, ua 
ranta ka anirangko onchapa. Sentenceni rokomrang, Sentenceni 
Bakrang aro Kattani Ma arangko ua Mesokanirang baksa talata. 
-be, -ai, -tok, -tel aro uagita katta bisemsemrangara Napsikgipa 
Adverb ine talata. Phrase, Clause aro Sentenceni Ma arangkoba 
diagram dake noksa sale talatani gimin bi sarangna masina 
altubea. 
Simple Sentenceko Bringija Sentence, Complex Sentenceko 
Donbrina Sentence aro Compound Sentenceko Mitapgipa 
Sentence ine ua dona. 
Phillips, Objective Marker ba Objectko Mesokgipa ‘-ko’ katta

tongko jakkalani giminba talata. A*chikkuo -ko katta tongko 
Objectnasan gatdapaija; Objectko talatgipa katta ba Adjectiveoba

6. Robbins Burlingni “A Garo Grammar” 
Robbins Burling Ki-taptangni gimin indake agana: “Origin- 
ally written as a by-product of anthropological field work in the 
Garo Hills between October, 1954 and October, 1956”. Burling 
iako Grammar sena miksongchong-mote sing sandie nie sea 
ong'ja. Indiba 1954 bilsini October jaoni 1956 bilsini October 
jaona, chong-motan bilsignina Garo Hillso re-roroe 
56 
Anthropologyni bidingo singsandianiko dakmitingo, 
A chikrangni aganani, katta gamatanirangko knae ragiminkosa 
ua ia Kitapo sea. Ian A chikkuo seaniba ong ja; Englishosa sea. 
Phonetics aro Phonemicsni bidingo janapchenganio Burling 
agana: “Garo can be described as having 21 segmental phonemes, 
5 vowels and 16 consonants. In addition there are important 
features of juncture*’. Burlingni jakkalgipa kattarangni gimin 
masie rachengna.

(a) Phonetics : Agananio gam anirang (speech sounds), 
uarangni gamaniko maidake ong katata (production), gamaniko 
knaani ba ra chakani (reception), gam anirangko nangdimatani 
(combination), talatani (description), ma mal dakani 
(classification) aro seani chinrangchi mesokani (representation 
by written symbols) , iarangko sulsul name sandie nianiko 
phonetics ine agana. 
(b) Phoneme: Kusik mingsao, aganani gamanirang jerangan 
kattarangko mingsa minggipinoniko dingtangata aro ortokoba 
maekata, uarang mingprakan phoneme ge prak onga. ‘Kam’ aro 
gam’ kattarango, katta a bachengao donggipa consonantni 
ganranirang dingtanga. Katta minggnini gnigipa aro gittamgipa 
gam-anirangde apsansrangaia. ‘k’ aro ‘g’ -ni dingtange gam*anichisa 
orto dingtanganiko ra baa; indake ‘k’ aro ‘g’ phoneme ge*gni ong a. 
Cha’ aro ‘chi’ kattarango ‘a’ aro T vowelrangni gam ani 
dingtanggrikani gimin, “cha” aro “chi” kattarang orto 
dingtanggrika. Kattarango orto dingtanganiko ra baani gimin, ‘a’ 
aro ‘i’ phoneme -rang ong a. 
(iii) Phonemics: Ku sik mingsani phonemerangko name 
sandie niani aro tik ka aniko Phonemics ine agana. 
(iv) Juncture: Agananio gam ani mingsani minggipin dake 
gam-angskaaniko aro gam-angskaani bewalko “Juncture” ine 
agana. Jekai: ‘b’ -ko rake balwa wate gam atode ‘p’-ni gam ani 
ongangskaa. 
Burlingni janapgipa kosako on gipa kattarang Ku-sikni 
bidingo sandie nianio jakkalgipa kattarangsa. Iarangna agreba, 
ua Morphology ba katta rikani bewal aro Syntax ba kattarangko 
bang e jakkaldimani ba sentence rikani bewalni giminba aditan 
talata. 
57 
Jakkalgipa kattarang aro segimin bakrangko nie, Burlingni 
Grammar Ki'tapara Grammarni niamrangni giminde, uni 
janapsoani gitan, ongbreja ine nika. Ia Kitap Achikkuo aganani 
gamanirangko sandie niani bidingo ong'bata. Ia Ki'tap school 
aro collegeni gadangrango ranade chudugaa. Indiba English

Honours/ Major poraigiparangna aro Universityo Linguistics 
ra giparangna aro Ku sikni bidingo sandirikkitgiparangnade 
cholibegipa ong'skagen. 
7. Robbins Burlingni “The Language of the Modhupur Mandi 
(GARO) Vol. I : Grammar” 
Burlingni ia Ki'tap 2004 bilsio nakata. Ki-tapni a-palni 
Kruanio indake sea: “This book is by far the most complete des- 
cription of the Garo Language ever published. Its focus is on the 
dialect of Garo that is spoken in Bangladesh and that is called 
‘Mandi’ by its speakers. All dialects of Garo are mutually 
intelligible, however, and most of what is described here is true 
of the dialects spoken in the Garo Hills as well. Where the dialects 
differ, Burling explains what these differences are. The book 
describes all aspects of the pronunciation and grammar of the 
language”. Englisho seani gita, ia Ki'tapko Bangladesho agangipa 
A-chikku Mandi ingipao gachate sea. Dingtang dingtang bipek 
A'chikkuko agangiparang ma-sigriktokaia aro ia Ki'tapo 
talatgipaan gipin kubipekrangnaba apsan ongaia ine iano janapa. 
Ma-sisretani nakatjana gita, Burling bipek Ku'sikrangko 
man-gope, sena jakkalgipa ku-sikko ‘Garo” ine ia Ki'tapo on-a; 
uni sandienggipa Ku-sikko ‘Mandi’ aro a'brini A-chikrangni, 
chongmotan Garo Hillsni A-chikrangni agangipako A-chik’ ine 
ua talata. Mandi aro A-chikara Garoni ku-bipekrang ine, ua 
ma ekgrikate jakkala. 
Ia Ki'tapko dolgittamna cholina gita sea ine ua agana: Ling- 
uistics-ni scholarrangna, chong motan Ku-sikni bidingo name 
ning-tue sandie nigipa aro uibegiparangna; A-chikkuko Gnigipa 
Ku-sik dake aganna skie ra-na sikgiparangna / nangnikgiparangna; 
A-chik ong-gijagipa a-palni mande jemangan A-chikkuko maidake 
talata uko uina sikai. A-chikrang an tangtangnan. Ia dolgittam 
uni Ki-tapko maidake jakkalo bang-bata namgniko man-gen aro 
an'tangni uina nangnikgipako badia bako altue nikgen, ukoba 
Burling talatchenga.

Segmental Phonology Bak 2,0 nakata. Segment inode 
maikoba bakrang dake sualaoni baksako miksonga. Phonologyni

chon-a dalja 
S-clusters ba ‘s’ baksa gipin oikorrang dongchapgipa 
tokrangni gimin janape, ia tokrango vowel dongoba, uarangko 
gam-atjanaba donga ine Burling agana. Indake ua “skjok”, “skbo” 
ine on - a, jerangkon A chikkuo ‘sikjok’ aro ‘sikbo’ ine sea. 
A-chikkuo ‘s’ aro ‘k’-ko sedimna ga-akode, uni ja-mano vowel 
mingsade dongtelaia; jekai: skia, sko. Word stress ba kattako 
ga-jete aganani, sentence Intonation ba sentenceko sul sale 
aganani giminba ua ia Bako man-chapata.

gimin talata. Mesokna ongiparanga: Ongenganiko mesokna

bilmanani giminba sandie nia.

on - a:

-ko talatgipa katta aro ‘gisima’-ko verb gita ra a.

ba’ Donggipa Kattarangni bidingo, Mandio aro paroni

(iv) Mijenko anga rang-sa-ba grongkuja.

Burling nika . Iandakgipa kattaranga: ka -sa-pa-e, ke-ma ka--bo, 
mit-tel-a, sal-nam, wal-nam. Mande ra-aniko mesokna

changrobate bil bate mesokgija be-en gome, be-enni bakrangchian

donganio maming gualaniko nikja. Indiba uamang grongani ba 
watgrikani somoeode kusik ontelaia ine ua agandapa.

(iii) Aro chu ringnoa. 
(iv) Anga mi chajokming, indiba aro cha-na nangnoa.

Clause Coherence: Final Noun Suffixes ba Clauseni 
Srangani:Bon kamao Nounna sokchuanirang-ko talatna ua ‘-ba* 
aro ‘-de* sokchuanirangko ra a, maina iarang orto dingtanganiko

aro ‘-sa’ sokchuanirangde gam anisa dingtangaia ine ua agana.

mesoka: 
(a) Unmarked Clause Coupling ba Clauseko 
69 
Nangdimatgipako jakkalgija:

(b) ‘-e’, ‘-eming’, ‘-emina’-ko jakkale”: 
(i) A ba game cha'a. 
(ii) Bange jomeming, ia mande maame chuinga. 
(iii) Srisri ratemina chajok. 
(c) ‘Dake’-ko jakkalgipa Adverbrang: 
Ran totok dake jaba songbo. 
(d) ‘ine’-ko jakkale: 
Bia sokbakuja ine anga chanchia. 
(e) Infinitive ‘-na’-ko jakkale: 
Biade bol gaduna sapa. 
‘-kan’, ka-na’-ko jakkalgipa Pangchakgipa Clausena ongipa 
Mesokani mingsara: 
Pi sako mi cha-kana (ine) magipa agana.

Bengalioniko ra sroakon ine Burling chanchichipa. Ua 
mingantikon che eme talata, indiba iano bang'gija 
Mesokanirangkosan niaina: 
‘je’ : (i) Anga je rama gita i*anga, ua rama gitan ibanoa. 
(ii) Je mande cha-aming, ua mandein ringaming. 
‘ba’ : (i) Bako anga am a, uko bia ra-bainga. 
(ii) Anga batcha i-angaming, uacha saksa 
mandeba dongjaming. 
Kattarangko rajujagrikani bidingo, ‘Jasenni pisako chikgipa 
achak” ingipako katta ra jujae, “je achak Jasenni pi sako 
chikaming” ine seskana man’a ine Burling mesoka.

segipa agana . ftrakani Sentenceko katta sulsul donaniko jujaatgija

nangdimatako man a ine ua agana. Mesokaniara: 
Bia re-banoama re-bajanoama?

‘da - ’- Construction ba ‘da - ’-ko jakkale sentence rikanina 
on - gipa Mesokanirangara: 
(i) Ring da - ring, bia onarijok. 
[Whether (we) drink or not , he just gave (it).] 
(ii) Tangka man da man anga iangnoa. 
(Whether I get money or not, I will go.)

indonga sentencerangko rika, indiba “ringeng ringjaeng” inesa 
segenchim. 
Passive ba Daka-Kaako Mesokgipa: Burlingni chanchianio, 
Passiveko Mandio aro Garoni gipin ku bipekrangoba niksengja. 
Active : Cha ukokko polis dokaming. 
Passive : Cha ukok polischa doka man - e cha aming. 
An chingni jakkalenggipa Achikkuo Passive sentence 
rikanide komibea ine anchingba uia. 
Comparative with ‘-kal’, ‘-bat’ ba ‘-kal’ aro ‘-bat’ -ko Jakkale 
Tosusaani: Napsikgipa Adverbrangoni ‘-kal’-ko Mandio aro ‘-bat’- 
ko A chikkuo jakkalrongbata ine Burling agana. Mesokanirangoni 
mitamara: 
(i) Anga nang - na bate dal kala. 
(ii) Jongadarangni sa dakbata?

sikgi parang aro sandirikkitdape uina sikgiparang ia Ki'tapko gelna 
manjawa. Atchikape Achikkuko agangipa ong'jaoba, Burling 
A chik Ku sikko aro uni bidingo namen ning tue uie ramanchaaha 
aro uko an-chingna poraina-nikna man-gipa bimango on aha.

Rev. Peter Remaba 1980 bilsio “Mandi Ku-nam” (Mandi 
Grammar] -ko nakatataha. Ua Grammarna “Ku-nam” kattako 
jakkala. “Ku-niam” inesachimma uko tale ra na chol ong jajok. 
Mandi ku-sikko skina gita ia Mandi- English Grammarko ua seaha.

Kh gam anina K, Ph gam anina P aro Th gam-anina T-ko ia 
Ki-tapo jakkala, ine Rema janapchenga. Consonantrango “raka” 
(-) donga gita ua sea. Ua balwa watgija gam-atgipa ‘t’ aro balwa 
74 
rake wate gamatgipa ‘t’ -ko (aspirate) maekatna indake sea gita

tota. Mesokani gita: “kaa” kattao “raka’’-ko ‘k’-ni jamano totja, 
‘ka-’ katta tongni. jamanosa tota. Bakkandike gam atgipa 
vowelrangko ma*ekatna Rema ‘u’-ko indakgipa vowelni kosako 
sea. Alphabeto ua ‘ch’ aro ‘ng’ gamanirangko manchapata. 
Achikku Grammaro alphabeto iarangko onTongja. 
Mandio gender gegnisan, Mea aro Mechik, ine Kitapo sea. 
A chikkuo jakkalgipa Ramram Gender aro Gri Genderko janapja. 
“Inanimate objects are considered as either masculine or

feminine”, ine janapa; indiba janggi grigiparang, jekai, nok, roong, 
mai gender o ong'a mesokani dongja. “For animals and things, 
the gender is expressed by affixing the word ‘bipa’ for the male 
and ‘bima’ for the female”, ineba janapa. Matburingrangna ‘bipa’ 
ba bima -ko on'dape Mesokanirang donga, indiba bosturangna 
Mesokanirang dongja. Mande, doo, sal aro uagitarangko Mandio 
mai gendero dona maming janapani ba talatani dongja. 
A chikku Grammaro gitan Mandioba Case rokom 8 donga 
ine nika. Mesokanirangna sentencerangko Mandio arobaEnglisho 
on-a. Caserangko masiama masija nina Ranta Ka-atanio English 
sentencerangko on e Mandiona peskaatania skie ra giparangna 
baditana uie raaniko on-gen talna man-ja. Pronounrangko 
talatanioba Englishkon jakkalbata. Adjectiverangnaba Ranta 
Kaanio English sentencerangko Mandiona pe-skachina on-a. 
Gipin Katta Bichongrangnaba ia apsan bewalkon ja-rika. 
Remani seani gita, Mandio rokom gittam Verbrang donga: 
Active, Causative aro Neuter. Tense rokom chikkingko on-a: 
Simple Present, Simple Past, Present Progressive, Immediate Rast, 
Remote Past, Past Progressive, Perfect, Pluperfect, Simple Riture 
aro Immediate Future. Mingbri Moodrangko janapa: Indicative, 
Imperative, Infinitive aro Subjunctive. 
Verbrangko talatanio jajaanirang aditan nakata. Jekai: “In 
Mandi, the person and number of the Verb is expressed not by 
the form of the verb itself, but by the personal pronouns”. Englisho 
‘person’ aro ‘number’ -ni kri verbni bunang dingtang ong-a. Indake 
“Number’ aro ‘Person’-sa Verbni kosako bilmana. Mandioba 
indake ong*a ine anga chanchija. A-chikkuo subjectm person 
aro ‘number’- ni kri Verbni bimang dingtang ong-ja.Mandio seako 
75 
mode A-chikkuo sea gitan apsan ine nikna man-a. Ki-tapo on-gipa 
Mesokanirangoba ia apsan niamkon ja rika. Gipin jajaanirangara: 
“The simple past tense is formed by affixing ‘-aha* to the root 
verb”, ‘-ha’ katta tongsa past tenseko mesokgipa onga. “The root 
verb is found (formed?) by dropping the final ‘-a* of the simple 
present”. Iaba niam re bikpilatani ong a.

Voiceni gimin seanio, “In Mandi strictly speaking there is 
no passive voice”, ine Rejna sea. A-chikkuoba ia apsanaia. 
Mandio Adverbrangko aro Direct aro Indirect Speech aro Tense 
jakkalani A-chikkuming apsanaia. Affixrangko ba 
sokchuanirangko Rema namen talata aro Mesokanirangko 
bangen ona; indiba onjotgipa ba sikjotgipa, chong motan 
Infixrangkoba maekatgija, Affixrangmingan manchapataia. Affix 
gita Mesokgipa ‘-dra’, ‘-chong-’, ‘-be-’ aro gipinrang Infixrangsa. 
Remaba Postpositionkosa janapa. Uni on-gipa 
Mesokanirangoni mitamara: baksa, -gita, -mung, gimin, taltal aro 
kingking. Noun in Apposition, Gerundial Infinitive, Noun Infinite 
aro Complementko janapsrokaia. Bak XXI-o Mandio jakkalgipa 
Adverbna Mesokanirangkosan onaia. Bak XXII-o apsan dake 
Conjunctionna Mesokanirangkosan on aia. Ianoba ‘gita’-ko 
Conjunction ine on a. A chikkuo ‘gita’-ko Postposition dakesa 
ra a. Interjectionna aro Ordinary Conversation ba Ramram 
Agangrikanina Mesokanirangko on an baksana, chananirangko 
see mesoka. Bon kamgipa Bako ia Numeral Articlerangko 
on a:sak, mang, dot, rang, gong, ge, pong, king, ming, pang, pat, 
te, jora aro bol.

A CHIKKU GRAMMARNI BIMANGO GIPIN 
GRAMMARNI BILMANANI 
AGANCHENGANI: 
Grammar inon anxhing kusik masako jakkalani 
ni amrang ko uie skie raani ine masia. Ku sik ma sako aganani 
aro seani niamrang, kuagangipa kattarang aro kusikni bimangko 
name ja'pang jadil sandie nianikon Grammar ine agana. Garo 
Grammar inon Garorangni Ku sikni, Achikkuni kattarang aro 
ku'sik jakkalani niamrangko miksonga. 
Achik Grammarko ning'tue sandie niani aro uko rongtale 
separakani da odipet komibea. Beben Garo Grammar ine biming 
on e seanirang aro chappa ka atanirang aditan donga. Indiba 
iarangoni kingsaba, Robbins Burlingni kitapna agre, chugimik 
Axhikku Grammami gunrangko mesokja. 
I 
Sechenggipa Garo Grammar Ki'taprang 
A-chikkuko ritchachixhet jolo seanio parakchengaba aro 
indake Axhikku Grammarko sandie setote nianiba abachengaha. 
Axhikku Grammarko ba Grammami bidingrango seanirangko bilsi 
baksa ka mao on a: 
1. Garo Vocabulary (1788-89): John Elliott, Daccani 
Commissioner, Axhikku kattarangko tom chimonge niah'a. 
2. Garo Vocabulary (1800): R’ancis Hamilton, uni “Account 
of the District or Zilla of Rangpur”-ni Dokgipa Bidolo, Axhik 
kattarangko tomxhimonge seaha. Ia seanio ua gipin ku sikni 
kattarang baksana Axhik kattarangko tosusae niaha. 
3. Garo Vocabulary (1837): Assamona re bachenggipa 
Americani Baptist Missionary Reverend Nathan Brown, uni 
“Comparison of Indo-Chinese Languages” ingipa seanio Axhik 
kattarangko manxhapataha. Ia kattarangko Garo Hillso kam 
78 
ka enggipa Junior British Officer John Strongsa tom’chimonge 
Reverend Brownna on-ahachim. 
4. Garo Vocabulary (1849): Assamo English Education 
Officer ong'gipa William Robinson, uni “Notes on the Language

spoken by the various tribes inhabiting the valley of Assam and 
its mountain confines” ingipao A'chik kattarangko 
manchapataha. 
5. Garo Vocabulary (1849): B.H. Hodgson uni “On the 
Aborigins of North-Eastern India” ingipa seanio, Tibetan, Dhimal, 
Bodo aro Garo ku'sikrangko tosusagrike niaha. 
6. Garo Vocabulary (1867): Garo a dokko nirokna dongipa 
Ramnath Chakravorty English-Bengali-Garo kusikrangto tosusae 
seaha. 
7. Garo Vocabulary (1868): W.W. Hunter uni segipa “A 
Comparative Dictionary of the Languages of India and High 
Asia”-o B.H. Hodgsonni segipa A chik kattarangoniko raskae 
on tisa seaha. 
8. Phrases in English and Garo (1868): la ki'tapko Assamni 
Americani Baptist Missionary Reverend Miles Bronson chappa 
kaataha. Iaba Achikku kattarangnasan mikpakma nibataiachim. 
9. A Vocabulary of the Garo and Koch Dialects (1869): 
Garo Hills districtni skanggipa Deputy Commissioner W.H. 
Williamson ia ki'tapko chappa ka ataha. 
10. A Vocabulary based on Robinson’s and Williamson’s 
collections of Garo Vocabulary (1872): A chikrangni janggi

E.T. Dalton, ia antangni seaniko “Descriptive Ethnology of 
Bengal”, ingipao manxhapataha. 
11. Dictionary of Garo Language (1873): A chikrangna 
watatako man'gipa gnigipa Americani Baptist Missionary T.J.

2. E.G. Phillipsni “A'chik Grammar”: la Grammar ki'tapo 
English Grammarni bilmanani dongoba, bilsuja. Tense-ko 
mesokanio kitap segipani sandichenganiko rongtalen nika. Mi tarn 
English Grammar kitapo somoeko mesokna Tense rokomgniko 
jakkala- Present aro Past Tense. Skangode Riture Tensekoba 
onachim. Indiba Future Tensekoba Present aro Past Tenseni 
ningon mesokna manaiani gimin mi tarn English Grammar 
ki'taprango uko maeke mesokja. Phillipsni Garo Grammar ki'tapo 
Janapani Mood-ni ning-o, Tense ming 8-ko ona. Iano Onggni 
Tense aro Bak Ong'gni Tense-ko ma'ekate mesoka aro an Thing 
Achikrang iandake jakkalaba jakkala. 
English Grammarkon Achikku Grammarko seanioba 
ja kkalaianik o Prepositionko talatanio nika. English Grammaro 
Preposition Nounna skang re - a. (Pre= skang, position =biap). 
Phillipsni ki tapo mesokani gita ongipa banga Prepositionrangan, 
Preposition chongmot ongja. Kakket gitade, A chikku Grammaro 
Preposition dongja. Postpositionsa (Post= jaman; 
position = biap) do nga . Ki'tapo on'gipa mesokanirangoniko 
mingsako ra-na. “Wangaljana kingking A'chikrang badiaba 
biterangko chaja.” Kingking katta Wangala kattamingsa 
nangchapa aro uan Wangala kattani jamansa rea, skang ong ja. 
Uni gimin uko Postposition ine mingosa krabata. Indiba English 
Grammarko ja rikpae an chingde Preposition ine seaia aro ian 
gualani onga.

Robbins Burling A'chikku Grammarko ning-tue sandirikkite niaha

Grammarde ong’ja. Uni setaigipa Garo Grammarara Bangladesho

1961, o chappa ka atgipa ki tapni jak 9,0 janapgipa ritingsa 
gisik nangatani ong a. A chik onggijagiparang ia ritingko 
aganrongana: “The Garos have such a hard time keeping then- 
spelling of J and_r straight”. Angni nika gitade, seanioj aro r ko 
tik ongen seaia. Agananiosa adokni kri ia ge-gni oikorni 
gamaniko basakoba mitamrang on jujagrike gamata. English 
manderang an tangtangba vowelrangko gam ataniode a dokni kri 
dingtange gam ata.
