IV 
Structure ba Bimang/ Bikrok (Sentence Rikani Bewal)

Englisho Subject + Verb + Object (SVO) bimangko jakkala. 
Khasirangba ia SVO bimangkon uamangni ku sikko aganamo aro 
seanio jakkala. Bengali aro Assameseo Sub ject-f Object + Verb 
(SOV) bimangko jakkala. A chikkuoba SOV bimangko jakkala. 
Indake sentence rikani bewalo A chikku Grammar English 
Grammaroni dingtangbee. A chikkuo sentence rikani Bengali aro 
Assameseo sentence rikani bewalming apsanangbata. Indiba ia 
bewalko A*chikrang ia ku sikrangoniko ra gataha ine aganna 
man-ja. 
Jatantian kattarangko gipin ku’sikrangoniko ra*gata aro 
ra*sroa. Indiba, sentence rikani bewalko gipinrangoniko 
ra-gatrongja. Jatantini ku*agananio, kattarangko sentenceo sulsul 
donani niamtang bewaltang dongaia. Indaken A*chikkuoba 
sentence rikani bewalo ba structure-o ku gipinni bilmanani 
dongja.

5. English Grammar gitade, main clause aro subordinate 
clauseni verbrang apsan tense-o ongna nanga. A-chikkuode, main 
clause past tense-o ong-oba, subordinate clausede present tense 
-o ong na man aia. English sentence gesako rana: He said that 
he was hungry. 
On-gimin sentenceo, “He said” main clause aro “he was 
hungry" subordinate clause ong-a. Main clauseni verb said aro 
subordinate clauseni verb was gegnian past tense-o ong a. Ia 
sentenceko da-ode A chikkuo done niskana. 
12a ml okkria ins aganaha. 
He/She rice is hungry thus say + past marker 
Ia sentenceo, main clausera “Ua aganaha” aro subordinate 
clauseara “(ua) mi okkria”. Main clauseni verb aganaha past 
tense-o ong-a, indiba subordinate clauseni verb okkria present 
tense-o ong-aia. Minggipinara, indaka sentencena A-chikkuo

subject ge sakosan jakkalaia. A*chikkuo indake ong'ani gimin,

ongronga.

7. Mitam sentencerango, main clause aro conditional clause 
donga. Englisho conditional clauseo if kattako aro A-chikkuo 
-ode katta bichemko jakkala. Main clause aro conditional

If your teacher came now, what would you do? 
Ia sentenceo, “What would you do” ara main clause aro “if 
your teacher came now” ara conditional clause ong a. Main 
clauseni “would do” aro conditional clauseni “came” verbrang 
past tenseo ong a. Ia sentenceara chanchichipe agananisan 
ong aia. Apsan sentenceko A chikkuo niskana. 
Nang-ni skigipa da o re baode, na-a mai dakgenchim? 
Your teacher now come +if you what do + future 
marker + 
past marker

Did he go? 
He didn’t go.

He/ She go + past marker + past marker 
+ question marker

10. Englisho have kattako simple present tenseo jakkalbata. 
Badiaba obostarangosan uko -mg bimango having ine jakkalna 
man aia. Have kattako A chikkuo man a ortoko miksonge 
aganode /jakkalode, “ ing bimango, chong motan having ine 
jakkalna man-a. Indiba kattako ramram donga ortoko miksonge 
jakkalode, present progressive tenseko ba -ing ko on dape having 
ine jakkalna manja. Mesokani gita, “We aro having (possessing) 
a car soon”, ine seode/ aganode sentence tik ong aia. Indiba donga 
ortoko miksonge, “ I am having two sisters”, ine aganode sentence 
ong jajok. Uni gimin, have kattako jakkalanio simsakna nanga. 
A-chikkuode skanggipa verbna “mannasiengjok” aro gnigipa 
verbna “donga” -ko jakkalani gimin jajaani dongja. 
11. A chikkuo perfect tensena bate past tenseko 
jakkalrongbata. Batanggimin somoeo maiba daka/ ka ani 
chu sokjahaode (unfulfilled condition), Englisho verbko past 
perfect tenseo sea. Jekai: 
Had he gone earlier, he would have got the job. 
Subordinate clause “Had he gone earlier” ingipao, past 
perfect tenseko jakkala. Ia sentenceko A chikkuo donskae, tense 
jakkalanio dingtanganiko nina. 
(i) Ua senggnang re angode, (ua) kam 
He/She early go + if (he/she) job 
mangnokchim. 
get + future marker 4- past marker 
(ii) Ua seng-gnang re-anggenchimode, (ua) kam 
He/She early go + future marker (he/she) job 
-l- past marker + if 
93 
mangnokchim. 
get + future marker + past marker 
Englisho past perfect tenseo ong-gipa verbko, Achikkuo 
present tenseo ba future markerko on-chape past tenseo jakkala. 
Iani a-sel Englishko skie ra-enggipa A-chik bi-sa tenseko jakkalanio 
neng-nika. Ua ka-mao on-a gita sentence riknaba donga:

*If he went earlier, he would have got the job.

perfect tense had gone ni palo past tense went ko jakkalsretaniko 
nika.

TENSE ARO ASPECT 
Verbara daka-ka-ako mesokgipa katta ong-a. Englisho, Verb 
katta mingsana batoba, A-chikkuode katta mingsa ong-rongaia. 
Verb kattako Latin katta verbum-oniko ra-a, jeni ortoara katta 
mingsa. 
Bang-bata ku-sikrangon katta rikna katta tongrangko 
(morphemes) donanio aro kattarangko sentenceo sulsul donanio 
altuaja; maina Verbni Tense, Aspect, Mood, Number, Pferson, 
Gender aro uarang gitako parakna rokomari sentence rikanirang 
aro kattani bimang dingtanganirang donga. 
(i) Tense inon, Verb ba daka ka-ani mai somoeo ong-a, 
da-oma, batanggiminma, mikkangchi ong-nasiama mesoka. 
(ii) Aspect inon, Verbo daka-ka ani aro somoe re-aniko 
mangsonga; chongmotan, matchotani, dakeng-ka-engani, badita 
mute daka-ka-a aro daktai -ka taianiko mesoka. 
(iii) Mood inon, agangipa agananina ba knatimenggipana 
mai bewalko mesoka, janapasama, sing-engama ba ge-etenga, 
uko mesokaniko miksonga. 
(iv) Number inon, bang a bang-gijako mesokani aro uni kri 
kattarangni bimang dingtang onganiko mesokaniko miksonga. 
Jekai: Sangipa-mande; Bang-gipa- manderang

(v) Person- ara, aganenggipama, aganako knatimenggipama 
ba aganako man-enggipa mandema mesoka. Jekai : an ga. na-a. 
ua. 
(vi) Gendera ra Nounni mea, me-chik ba janggi gri onganiko 
mesoka. Jekai : me-asa. me-chik, bol. 
English Ku siko Verbrang nangrimgrike, Verbal group ba 
Verbni kamko ka-gipa katta tokko ong-ata. Jekai : live in ba 
dongrima, live on ba tangkua, live on ba tangna pangchaka aro - 
uagita. A-chikkuode indake ong-ja. Basakobade A-chikkup 
verbrangko agananio ba seanio watchanga. Mesokani gita, ‘'Uan 
dos dakgipa”. Sentence matchotjaoba, sentenceni ortokode 
97 
ma-siaia, maina Verb one a sepangon napchipile dongaia. 
Dingtang dingtang Ku-sikrang sentenceo kattarangni 
nangrimgrikaniko aro grammarni gita sentenceni miksonganiko 
parakna rokomari bewalrangko aro cholrangko jakkala. Ia seanio 
daka-ka-aniko somoeni (tense) aro daka-ka-anio somoeni reani 
(aspect) bidingrang aro A-chikkuo uarangko parakani cholrangko 
nia. Daka-ka-ani somoe aro somoeni re ani ekgrikatna man-pilgija 
nangrimgrika. Da-oni ba obostani somoe re-ani, chong-motan 
kamko ka-engama, ka-manahama, uko ma-ekate chanchina 
man ja. Somoeara a gilsak gimikni, kattaming-ku-sikming 
nangchapgijagipa chanchiani ong-a. Ua bak gittam donga. 
Batanggimin, Dao aro Mikkangchini. Tenseara Verbni bimangni 
aro somoeko an-chingni uiani aro ma-sianini nangrimgrikaniko 
mesoka. Verbni kamko maidake nike-uie ra-a ua bewalko Aspect 
mesokskaa. Gipin dake aganode, kamko ka-engkuama ba 
matchotaha uko Aspect mesoka.

MAIKO AGANA. 
A-chikkuo Grammar Ki-taprango Tense Systemni bidingo 
maiko janapa nie nina. Samson K. Sangmani A-chik Grammar, 
Lower Primary Classrangna miksonge seani gimin, Tenseni bidingo 
che-eme seja. Ki-tap segipa ia minggittam Tenserangko janapa: 
(i) Da o Ong-gipa Tense (Present Tense)

(ii) Ong-gimin Tense (Past Tense) 
(iii) Ong-gni Tense (Future Tense) * 
Da-o Ong-gipa Tenseni ning-o ua Da o Ong-enggipa Tense 
ba Present Progressive Tenseko janapa. 
E.G. Phillips “Outline Grammar of the Garo Language” 
ki-tapni Bak V-o indake sea: “There are three classes of verbs in 
Garo: The Neuter, Active and Causal. There are properly no 
Passive verbs, the passive idea being expressed by a peculiar 
form of sentence. The Verb is regulady conjugated, expressing 
by use of endings, moods and tenses”.

Mitam English Grammarko segiparang Past Tense aro Non- 
Past Tense ine jakkala. Ia minggni Tenseni ning-on Progressive 
(Dao Ong-enggipa) aro Future (Ong-gni) bidingrangko 
man-chapaia ine chanchianio indake jakkala. A-chikkuode 
Present, Past aro Future Tenserangkon ja-pang dake ra-a. Indiba

mesokna gipin Tenserangko on-chapa. Chong-motan, daka-ka-ani 
matchotkuja, ong-engkua ine mesokna Da o Ong-enggipa Tenseko 
jakkala. 
1. DA-O ONG GIPA TENSE (Simple Present Tense) 
Da-o ong-gipa somoe batanggimin aro ong-gni somoeko 
ma-ekatgipa ba jatchi tiktako bak dakgipa ong-a. “Da-o somoe” 
ua dikdikni ja-mantapan “batanggimin somoe” ong-aijok. “Da-o 
somoe” on-titi skangsan “ong-gni somoe” ong-achim. “Da-o 
somoe” inon ua dikdikko man-chapgipa somoekosan miksongaia. 
A-chikkuo ka-mao on-giparangko mesokna Da-o Ong-gipa 
Tenseko jakkala:

one-aia.

(b) Chekan chroka.

ong-a aro aspectni gita ua dikdikkon miksonga. 
(ii) Da-o ong-gipa obosta ba daka-ka-ani. iean batanggimin 
aro ong-gni somoeming nangchana.

2. ONG-GIMIN TENSE (SIMPLE PAST TENSE) 
Ong-gimin Tenseko jakkalanio ja-pang bidingrang minggni 
donga: (a) Janapenggipa daka-ka-ani da^o ong-gipa somoena 
skangan ong-aha. (b) la daka-ka-ani da-o ong-gipa somoena 
skangan badiaba somoe tiktako ba somoe manchao ong-aha. 
Gipin dake aganode, Ong-gimin Tenseara skangon matchotgimin 
daka-ka-aniko mesoka aro da-o ong-gipa somoekode 
man-chapatsrangja. Ong-gimin daka-ka-aniko, da-o ong-gipa 
somoeko man-chapgijagipa Adverbrangchi mesoka. Indakgipa 
Adverbrangara skangon . meialo . meiao. minio aro uarang gita. 
Somoeko mesokani baksa, talatchapgipa kattako jakkaleba, da-o 
ong-gipa somoeko man-chapatgija, batanggimin daka-ka-aniko 
mesokna man-a. 
Mesokani: Re-anggipa jao giljani antiko ong-ataha. 
Ia sentenceo “jao” Adverbmangmangchide daka-ka-aniara 
batanggimin somoeosa ine mesokna man-ja. “Jao”-ko talatgipa 
katta "re-anggipa”- sa batanggimin somoeko srange talata. 
Ong-gimin Tenseko mesokna A-chikkuo -ha. -jok aro - chim 
sokchuanirangko Verbna on-dapa. 
Mesokani: (a) Ua kamtangko mejalo matchotaha. 
(b) Ua kamtangko mejalon matchotjok. 
(c) Ua kamtangko mejalon matchotahachim. 
Sentence ge-gittaman Past Tenseo aro uarango batanggimin 
somoeko mesokgipa Adverb mejalo/ mejalon-ko jakkala. 
Skanggipa sentence kam matchotsranganiko kimkim agana. 
Gnigipa sentenceo kam matchotdaldalko mesoka. Iano kam 
matchota matchotjana simsakbreja; mejalon inanichi somoe nasa 
mikpakma nia ine uina man-a. Gittamgipao -ha baksana -chim 
sokchuaniko on-anichi maiba orto matchotgijaniko mesoka.

Aspectba batanggimin somoekon mesoka.

(i) Batanggimin somoeni obosta ba daka-kaani, jeon 
somoeko ianapa ba ianapia.

(b) Ua ia biaponi skangon jitangaha.

gnigipa sentenceo “skangon” Adverb dongani gimin jitani ruutaha 
inesan uiaia; indiba gittamgipa sentenceode, mai bilsi manchao 
jita srangen mesoka. 
(ii) Basakomanchako mesokgiia. ong gimin daka-ka ani 
iean da-ode dontongaha . 
Mesokani : (a) Amkang uno bilsi dok poraiaha. 
(b) Simkan Turao bilsi chet dongaha. 
Sentence ge-gnini Verbrang poraiaha aro dongaha Past 
Tenseo. Aspectni gita, ua daka-ka ani da-o ong jaengjok, 
dontongaha; uni gimin batanggimin somoeko mesoka.

da-ode uno poraijajok aro Simkan Turao dongachim, da-ode gipino 
dongengjok ine ma-sina man-a.

Mesokani : (a) Ua be-en ranta ka-rongaha. 
(b) Baba seng-gnang meatam cha-rongaha.

8. SKANG ONG-GNI TENSE fPast Future Tense) 
Skang Ong-gni Tenseko A-chikkuo jakkalja. 
Mingsamangmang bidingna ia Tenseko jakkala aro uara mande

dongtaia. Uni gimin Skang Ong-gni ba Past Future Tenseo Verb 
ge-gnian ga-aka. Aspectde, mikkangchini ba ong-gni somoean 
ong-aia. 
Iandake jajaatgipa Verbrang dongkua. “Uamangba 
re-angenggen” sentenceo, - eng - ong-enganiko aro - gen

sokchuaniara ong-gni somoeko mesoka. Sentenceni 
miksonganide chanchichipe agananisan ong-aia. “Uamang 
re-angmanenggnok” sentenceo, -man-skang ong-giminko ba 
perfectiveko mesoka; - eng- ong-enganiko ba progressiveko 
mesoka; - gnok- bakan ong-gniko ba immediate futureko mesoka. 
“Re-angmanenggnok” inaniba tik uisranggija rim-chipe agananisa 
ong-bata. 
Iandaka sentenceo -gen katta tongko jakkalja; jakkalode 
sentence ong-gniko mesokgija orto dingtang ong-a. On-gimin 
mesokanio, - gnok ong-gniko mesokoba, chanchichipe ra-anikosa 
mesokbata.

aro ortorangko Ku-gipinona ra-gatskarongaia. Ku-sik mingsani 
niamrangko Ku-gipinni niamrangming apsanatrongani bewalba

ka-engo, uni Ama-Ku-ni niamrang peng-panga aro bi-sao jajaani 
nakata. Ku-sik minggnini Grammarko name nie ra-chengjaode, 
skigipaba classo skianio neng-nikaniko chagronga. Indiba

7. Past Perfect Progressive Tense 
Present Perfect Progressive Tense gitan, Past Perfect 
Progressive Tenseko A chikkuo jakkalrongja. 
Nipiltaie nie, A-chikkuoba Simple Present Tenseko jakkalani 
donga ine aganna man-a aro uan Da-o Ong-gipa Tense. Indiba 
Englisho gita, an-ching badiabarangna Present Tenseko jakkalgija, 
Present Progressiveko jakkalskaa. Jekai : “The food smells good” 
inode, an-ching “Cha-ani similenga”, ine aganbata. Uni ortoara, 
pe-skaaniode Tense dingtangataniba dongaia. Clauserangko 
jakkalanioba ia apsan Tense dingtanganiko nika. Chong-motan, 
A-chikkuo, Ja-pang Clause aro Pangchakgipa Clauseni Verbrang 
apsan Tenseo ong-rongja.

Simple Past Tenseko Achikkuo Ong-gimin Tense ine jakkala. 
Englisho Verbni Past Tense inahaode jujaani dongjajok. Jekai:

Present- eat, Past -ate; Present - do, Past -did. Indiba Englisho 
Verbantini Past Tense bimang dingtang ong-a. A-chikkuode 
chinchisa, jekai -gjijffi ba -Jia, batanggimin somoeko mesoka.

nangaia. Minggipinara, Englisho Verb Past Tenseo ong-oba, 
A chikkuo donskaon Present Tenseko jakkala. 
Englisho Present Progressive Tense ingipara A chikkuo Dao 
Ongenggipa Tense. A chikkuo Da o Ongenggipa Tenseo, daka- 
ka-anini ong-enganiko mesokna gita, Verbni ning-o -eng - katta 
tongko sikjota. Englishora -ing katta tongko Verbna sokchuskaa. 
Present Progressive Tense Englisho apalbata aro uko 
jakkalani rokomrang bang-bata. Jekai, ong-taitaianiko mesokon, 
Englisho ia Tenseko jakkala. A-chikkuode indaka daka -ka-anina 
Present Tenseko jakkalbata. Jekai, “You are always asking for 
money” ingipana, A chikkuo Present Tenseko jakkale indine sea: 
“Na-ade tangka bi-simaia”. 
Past Progressive Tense ba Skang Ong-enggimin Tensekoba 
A chikkuo jakkala. Indiba dakronggimal ka-ronggimal kamrangko 
mesokanio, A chikkuo daktaitaianiko mesokna -rong -katta 
tongko Verbni ning-o sikjote, Past Tenseko jakkala. Indaka 
sentenceo Past Tenseko mesokna -ha sokchuaniko on-gija, - chim 
sokchuaniko on-bata. 
Present Perfect Tense ba Da o Ong-sogimin Tensena bate 
A chikkuo Past Perfect Tense ba skang Ong-sogimin Tenseko 
jakkalbata. Englisho “I have taken tea” inon, have taken Present 
Perfect Tense ong-a. A-chikkuode, “Anga cha ringmanjok” inon - 
man katta tongko jakkale skang ong-aniko mesoka aro -jok 
sokchuaniara ong-gimin ong-oba ong-daldalaniko mesoka. Indake 
ua Immediate Past Tenseosa ong-a. 
Agangimin gita, A-chikkuo Past Perfect Tense ba Skang 
Ong-sogimin Tenseko jakkalbea. Present Perfect Progressive Tense 
ba Skang Ong-soenggimin Tenseko A-chikkuo jakkalja. Past 
Perfect Progressive Tensenaba ia apsan neng-nikani donga.

Mande ku-aganbaon Kuamako aganbata. Gipin A-songo 
atchigiparang ba ma-a paa bringipa jatrangni dedrang ua a-so ngni 
ba ma-a ba paani Ku amako agannaba donga. Unonikosa 
Ku-gipinko skie raa. Ku-sikantian sentencerango kattaran gko 
sulsul orto dongna gita dona aro grammami gita sentenceni 
mangsonganiko parakna dingtang dingtang chol-bewali angko 
jakkala. Kattarangko tik onge dona, jedakode sakantian uko tik 
onge dongen, jakkalgen aro indake Ku-sikko aganon ba seon 
pilakan ma-sigen. Ia indake dongimin niamrangko Gr ammar ine 
agana. Sentence-o kattarangko bilsi bil-dak donaiode, saksa 
sakgipinni aganako ba seako ma-sijawa. Ku-sik skie ra-a inon ia 
niamrangko, kattarangko aro kattarangni ortoko skie ra a. 
Ku-gipinko skie ra-on, skie ra-enggiparang Ku-amani 
Grammami niamrang, bimang bikrok aro sentence rikani bewalko 
gisikoniko chel-atja. Ku-amani niamrangko aro ku-gitalni 
niamrangko, chanchimanchae ong-jaoba, uamang apsangopatna 
jotton ka-a. Ku-gitalko skie bil si bil-dakata. Indake Ku-gitalko 
skie ra-anio Ku-amani Grammami bilmanani dongtelaia. Ku-amani

niamrangko Ku-gitalo ra-gatna jotton ka-on ma-sisretanichi 
gualanirang nakata. 
Gipin Ku-sikrang maidake re-a aro kam ka-a uko name sandie 
nigiparang, bang-a Ku-sikrangni grammami gita 
dingtanggrikanirangko uina, uamangni rokomari bidingrangko 
tosusae pe-rarone nia. Iako dakanichi badiaba Ku-sikko skie 
ra-enggiparangni Ku-gitalko ku-agananio aro seanio gualanirangko 
agansamsona aro maini gimin ua gualanirangko daka uko talatna 
man-a. Ia agansamsoaniara skigiparangna aro skie 
ra-enggiparangnaba dakchakbeani ong-a. 
A-chik bi-sarang English-ko skie ra-on A-chikkuni 
b ilmananichi English-ko aganani aro seanio guala nira ngko daka. 
Ia angni seaniara indakgipa gualanirangni bidingo ong-a.

bewalrangko sandisokja. Indake, mitara gualanirangkosan ia

sentencerang ba sentencerangni bakrang donga. Tik ong gijagipa 
ba gualani donggipa sentencerangni ba sentencerangni bakrangni 
a-bachengao asterisk (*) chinko on e uarangko ma ekata.

Englishkosa jakkalbaengani gimin iano seani British Grammar

Gualronggiparang ba Sesretanirang: 
An-chingni bi-sarang Englishko Gnigipa Ku-sik aroba Kugital 
gita skie ra-a. A chikku aro Englishni bimang bikrok, sentenceo 
katta donani aro verbrangko jakkalani niamrang dingtanggrikbea. 
Sentenceo katta donani bikrok indake ong a: 
A-chikku : Ua bibalrangko aka. (Subject + Object + Verb 
ba SOVJ 
English : She plucks the flowers. (Subject + Verb + Object 
ba SVO) 
Ramrambegipa sentencerang. Indiba sentence bikroko,

Bang-gipa Numberko jakkalani chinrango, Sangipa Numberna 
Verb jakkalanio aro Objectko mesokna chin on-anio 
dingtanganiko nika. Achikkuo Sangipa Numbernaba, Bang-gipa 
Numbemaba Verb “aka” -an choliaia. Englisho “she” Third Person 
Sangipa Number ong-ani gimin verb “pluck” -na “s” -ko ondapa. 
A chikkuo Object "bibalrang”-o Object ine mesokna “-ko” katta 
tongko on dapa; indiba Englisho Object “flowers” -na indake 
on-dapani dongja. Indake dingtanggrikanirangni a-sel, bi-sarang 
jajaanirangko aro neng-nikanirangko man-a. Jajaanirang sokon, 
skie ra-enggipa bi-sarang Ku-amani niamrangko Ku-gitalo ra-gate 
agana aro sea. Unon gualanirang aro sesretanirang ong-kata, 
jerangkon Ku-sikni bang-a bidingrangon nika. Janapgimin gita, ia 
bidingrangoni aditakosan ia seanio ra-aia.

Sentenceo Kattarangko Sulsul Donani: 
Sentenceni bikrokde Subject+Object+Verb ine nikmanaha. 
Sentencerango kattarangko sulsul donani A-chikkuo 
olgrokbata. Mesokanina: 
Na-a angna iano sengbo. 
Angna na-a iano sengbo. 
Na-a iano angna sengbo. 
Iano na-a angna sengbo. 
Kosako on-gipa sentencerango kattarangni sulsul re-aniko 
donjujaoba, ja-pang ortode, kosakgilgil mangsonganide apsanaia. 
Name niosa dongnue donggipa ortokode nikaia. Jekai: 
Na-a (gipin ong-ja) angna iano sengbo. 
Angna (gipinna ong-ja) na a iano sengbo. ' 
Na-a (gipin ong-ja) iano (gipino ong-ja) angna sengbo. 
Iano (gipino ong-ja) na-a angna sengbo. 
Englisho indake olgroke sentenceni kattarangko jitrurae rona 
man-ja. Jekai: 
Wait for me here. 
*Here wait for me. 
*Me for wait here. 
*Here for me wait.

*Wait me for here. 
Bon-kamgipa sentence gittamni ortoko ma-sina neng-a. 
Kattarangko jebasi donaiode, sentenceni orto dongja. Uni gimin 
Sentenceo kattarangko tik ong-e sulsul donani mongsonganiko 
bi-sarang tale uie ra-na nanga. 
A / An / The Articlerangko Jakkalani: 
Englisho Nounrangko mesokna aro uarangko jakkalaniko 
ma-ekatna a, an aro the -ko ba articlerangko jakkala. Iarangoni a 
aro an-ara indefinite articlerang aro the -ara definite article ong-a. 
Uarangko basako ba badiaming jakkala uarangna English 
Gramm aro donsogimin niamrang donga. Englisho nounrangna, 
consonantni gam-anichi a-bachenggipa kattar angna skang JL*ko 
on-a. Vowelni gam - anirhi a-bachenggipa nounrangm mikkangode 
141 
an-ko sea. Jekai: 
(i) a + consonantni gam-ani (ii) an + vowelni gam-ani 
a dog an egg 
a flower an ant 
a,e,i,o,u vowelrang ong-a. Iarangoni e,o,u vowelrang 
ong'oba, uarangko basakobade Englisho consonantrang gita aro 
semi-vowelrang gita gam-ata. 
Jekai: a European 
a one-way street 
a university 
“European” katta “e”-chi a-bachengoba aro “university” katta 
“u” -chi a-bachengoba, uarangko semi-vowel dakesa gam-ata. 
“one-way-street" katta “o” vowelchi a-bachengoba, uko an-ching 
“w” gitasa gam-ata. Uni gimin ia kattarangna skang “a” -kosa 
sea. 
Da-ode “hour” kattako niskana. “Hour” katta “h” 
consonantchi a-bachenga; indiba uko gam-aton, “h” ko 
gam-atgija, gnigipa oikoronisa, jean vowel ong-a, gam-ata. 
Chong-motan, *“a hour”ine agangija / segija, “an (h)our” ine agana / 
sea. Indiba pilak “h” oikorchi a-bachenggipa kattarangko 
gam-aton “h” -ko galna nangade ong-skaja. A-chikkuode nounna

skang articlerangko on-chengna nangjani gimin, Englisho “a” aro 
“an” ko jakkalna nangon jajaa. “Hour” gita dakgipa kattarangko 
chagrongon "h” gnang gam-ataia aro *“a hour” ine segualaia. 
Vowelni gam-anichi a-bachenggipa oikorrangara 
a,e,f,h,i,l,m,n,o,r,s,x. Gipin oikorrangde consonantni gam-anichi 
a-bachenga. Englishchi chananioba, 8,11, aro 18 vowelni 
gam-anichi a-bachenga. “m” gita dakgipa oikorrangko 
consonantni aro vowelni gam-anichi a-bachengna man-a. Uni 
gimin, ‘a minister’ inna man-oba, ‘an M.L.A’. inosa tik ong-skaa. 
Una agreba, ‘I need an eight to complete the puzzle’ ine aganosa/ 
seosa tik ong-a. Bi-sarang articlerangko jakkalanio gualjana gita, 
iarangko tosusagrike talate on-na man-a. 
Englisho uncountable nounrangna ba channa man-gijagipa 
nounrangna "a" ba "an” -ko jakkalja. Indakgipa nounrangoni 
mitamrangara: beauty, poetry, advice, information. 
142 
“A girl” ba “girls” inon je mechik bi-sakoba ba mechik 
bi-sarangko miksonga. Indiba “the girl” inahaode, ua me chik 
bi-sani gimin janapgimin dongaha aro badiaba me chik bi-sakosa 
mangsonga ine uina man-a. Iarangko aganate, seate ranta 
ka-jringatosa sesretani ba gualani komigen. 
Bang-gipa Number 
A-chikkuo Bang-gipa Numberko mesokna no unna “-r ang ” 
katta tongko on-dapa. Jekai: bi-sarang. Chananiko on-chapgipa 
katta dongode, “-rang”-ko on-dapjaoba ong-aia. Englisho 
Bang-gipa Numberko mesokani cholrang bang-a. 
(a) Englisho “s” -ko nounna on-dape Bang-gipa Numberko 
mesoka. Jekai: cows, flowers, boys, houses. 
(b) Basakobara “s” na skang “e” oikorko on-e, Bang-gipa Number 
daka. Jekai : watches, matches, peaches. Iano nika, katta 
“ch”-o bon-ode, “es” ko on-dape Bang-gipa Number daka. 
(c) Mitam nounrangni bang-ako mesokna gita, kattani 
bon-kamachipakgipa oikorrangko dingtangate, “s” ba “es” - 
ko on-dapa. Jekai : baby-babies; shelf-shelves; wife-wives; 
leaf-leaves.

(d) Mitam nounrangnara, jatchini vowelko on-sree, Bang-gipa 
daka. Jekai : man-men; woman-women. 
(e) Mitam nounrangde Sangipa Numbernaba Bang-gipa 
Numbernaba bimang dingtangja. Indakgipa nounrangoni 
mitamara deer, fish, sheep. 
(f) Mitam nounrangde Sangipa Numbero aro Bang-gipa 
Numbero namen dingtanggrikbea. Jekai : louse-lice; mouse- 
mice; medium-media; stratum-strata; phenomenon- 
phenomena. 
Ia ma-atanirangko ma-sijae ba gisik ra-sokjae aro A-chikkuo 
“-rang -ko on-dapa gita “s”- ko on-dapon choliaigen ine 
chanchisrete, segualanirangko daka. Jekai: watch-*watchs; baby- 
*babys; fish-* fishes; mouse-*mouses. 
Ia ramram nounrang baksana, nounrangko channa man-gipa 
(countable), channa man-gijagipa (uncountable) aro dondimgimm 
143 
(collective) nounrang dake ma-mal dakani donga. Channa 
man-gijagipa nounrangoni mitamrangara: milk, water, bread, 
advice, dirt, knowledge, scenery, furniture. Channa man-gijagipa 
nounrangna “s” -ko on-dapja. “a piece of advice", “two loaves of 
bread”, inesa jakkala. Beauty-ni orto “nitoani”; Bang-gipa 
Numberko mesokna “beauties” ine seode “silsilgipa nomilrang” 
inesa ong-skajok; chong-motan kattani miksongani 
dingtangsrangjok. 
Dondimgimin Nounrang Sangipa Numberni bimango ong-a. 
Indakgipa mitam nounrangara: crowd, class, team, staff, company, 
committee, population. Iarangna basakoba Sangipa verbko 
basakoba Bang-gipa verbko jakkala. 
Mitam nounrang Bang-gipa Numberni bimango ong-kamaia. 
Jekai: glasses, spectacles, sunglasses, goggles, trousers, jeans, 
pyjamas, shorts, slacks, scissors, mathematics, measles, news. 
Ia kattarangoni mitamrang Bang-gipa verbko jakkala. Jekai: 
“These trousers are too tight for me”. Indiba, indakgipa mitam 
kattarang Sangipa verbko jakkala. Jekai: “Mathematics is a 
difficult subject”.

A-chikkuo srongsrong seani ja-mano, Englishchi bang-gipa 
nounrangko niani, bano “s”-ko on-gen, bano mamingkon 
on-dapna nangja, una tiktaksranggipa niam dongja. Uarangko 
gisik ra-ani cholde jakkaljringanisan ong-aia. 
Minggipin an-chingni bi-sarangni gualaniko nounko talatani 
katta gita jakkalanio nika. Katta tok gittamko nina : a two-month 
old child; ten-rupee notes; thirty-minute exercise. An-chingni 
bi-sarang, jagni ong-ode, “two months”, gong-chikking ong-ode 
“ten rupees” aro minit kolatchi ong-ode “thirty minutes” ong-gen 
ine ma-sisreta. Indaken, *“a two-months old child”, * “a ten- 
rupees note” aro ‘"thirty -minutes exercise” ine ong-gija sea. 
Bang-gipa ong-ani chinko sentenceni subjectnasa aro nounnasa 
on-dapa. Iano “two-month”, “ten-rupee” aro “thirty-minute” katta 
tokranga, “child”, “note” aro “exercise” nounrangko talatgipa 
kattarangsa ong-a; talatgipa kattarangnade bang-ani chin “s”-ko 
on-dapna nangja. Bi-sarangna iako name talate on-ode, uamang 
gualaniko dakjawa.

Bang-bata Ku’Sikrangon Verbrangko morphemerangona,

nengniktoka. Englisho tense, aspect, mood, number, parson aro 
uarang gitako parakna rokomari katta rikani aro sentence rikani 
bewalrangko jakkala. Grammami gita, katta rikani aro sentence 
rikani bakrang rokomari bewalrango ge-sa ge gipinming 
nangbakgrika, nangkapgrika aro bilmangrika. Englisho verbrangni 
nangkapgrikaniko nambatgipa mesokanide verbal group ba verbal 
phrasean ongaia. Verbal phraserangko ja mano kan dike nina. 
Je ong oba, subject aro verbni meligrikna nangani a-selan, verbni 
bidingo bang-a gualanirang nakata. 
Verbko Jakkalani ba Galchangani: 
Verban sentenceo mongsonggipa kamko kaa. Basakobade

ong on, verbrang gimasrangade ongja, napehipbile dongasa ba

ramram galchangja. Indiba A-chikkuo sentencerang verb griba

basakobade “Peterde ukilsa” inanina, * “Peter a lawyer” ine, verb 
“is” -ko galchange sea aro ian gualani ong chongmota. 
A chikkuode “Peter ukilsa (ong-a)” ine ma-siaia. Indiba Englisho 
“Peter a lawyer” inode “lawyer”- ara Peterko talatgipa kattasa 
ong aia aro verbara maian ong genchim uisokja. 
Verbni Bimang Dingtangani: 
A-chikkuo Number, Person aro Genderna verbni bimang 
dingtang ong ja. Subjectko ba uko talatgipako niesa bang-ama 
bang-ja, agangipama knatimgipama aro me-asama me chikma 
uiaia. Ka mao mesokanirangko nina. 
A-chikku: (i) Bi-sa grapenga. 
(ii) Bi-sa sakgittam grapenga. 
(iii) Bi-sarang grapenga.

English: (i) The baby is crying. 
(ii) Three babies are crying. 
(iii) The babies are crying. 
A-chikkuo, bi-sa saksanoba, bi-sa bang-oba verbni bimang 
apsanaia- "grapenga”. EngHshode, saksana “is crying” aro bang-ana 
“are crying" ine jakkala; chong-motan numberni kri verbni 
bimang dingtang ong-a. Minggipinara, Englisbo Bang-gipa 
Numbemade “s” ba bang-ako mesokgipa cbinko on-tebia nangaia.

Sangipa; baditako talatgipa kattachisa Bang-gipa ine uiaia.

inani gimin Englishoba * “She go” inon ong-susu nikani gimin 
indaken seaia aro gualaniko daka. 
Auxiliary Verb “do”:

“Do”-ni gipin bimangrangara does , did , done aro doing. “Do” 
verb dakchakpagipa verb ong-oba, basakobade uko verb mancha 
gitan jakkala. Jekai : “He does the work”. Present Simple Tenseo, 
subject third person singular number ong-on, an-ching does/ does 
not/ doesn’t-ko jakkala. Gipin subjectrang baksade, do/do not/ 
don’t-ko jakkala. 
English-o “do” katta sing-anirango aro jechakani 
sentencerango mongsonggipa biapko ra-a. A chikkuode indaka 
katta dongja. “Ua basako siaha?” ine Verb “siaha"-ko joljol 
jakkalaia. Englishode “do” ni dakchakaniko ra-e “When did he 
die?” ine jakkala. “siaha” Past Tenseo ong-a: “die” Verbkode “did” 
-ni dakchakachisa past tense dakata. Skie ra-giparang, A-chikkuo 
seani bimangko ja-rike basakoba, * "When he died?” ine seguala. 
Negative ba jechakani donggipa sentenceo, A-chikkuo jechakani 
chin “-ja” -ko Verbna on-dapa. Jekai: “Ua name seja”. Englishode 
“do” -ni dakchakako ra-esa "He doesn’t write well” ine sea. Iano 
“write” Verbko baibai see, negative markerkode “do” nasa on-jota. 
Iarangko tik ong-e sena gitade ranta ka-beaniko nanga. 
A-chikku “-eng” aro English “-ing” -ko Jakkalgijagipa Verbrang: 
Daka-ka-anini ong-enganiko mesokna A-chikkuo Verbna 
“-eng” -ko aro Englisho - “ing”- bimangko jakkalgijagipa adita 
146 
Verbrang donga. Uarangoni mitamara: bear, smell, feel, know 
have. Mesokanirangko adita nina . ’ ’ ’ 
Meatamna song-gipa cha-anini biba kutturi gipinoni 
balbaode, A-chikkuode “Bijak similbaenga” ine agana. Englishode 
apsanko, “The dinner smells good” ine agana. Indiba A-chikkuo- 
“eng”- ko jakkalani gimin, Englishchi seoba *“The dinner is 
smelling good” ine sea aro ian gualani ong-a. Apsan dake, *“I am 
hearing a nice song" ba *“I am feeling okay”, ine sentence 
rikanirangba tik ong-ja. Verbo “-ing” -ko on-dapna nangja. 
Englisho "have” kattara rokomari ortoo jakkalna man gipa 
katta ong-a. Uarangoni mingsara “possess” ba “donga”. Gari 
brenasiengode, Englisho “I’m having a car soon” ine agana. 
Sentenceko nion aganenggipa mandeo gari dongkuja, indiba

ru-utgijan donggen ine ma-sina man-a. Una agreba, somoeko 
mesokgipa katta ba Adwerb “soon”, man giminko donggiminko 
mesokja, mikkangchi donggnikosa mesoka. Indaka obostaode 
“have” na “-ing” bimangko jakkalani gimin, donggimin 
man-giminnaba, apsan ong-aigen ine chanchisrete, mi tam skie 
ra-enggiparang “having” kattako jakkale sentence rika. Jekai: 
*“I am having a house in Tura”. “I have a house in TUra” ine 
seosa sentence tik ong-a.

Tense System: 
A-chikku aro Englishni tense systemrang badiabao apsana, 
banobara dingtangani gimin Englishko skie ra-giparang jajaa aro 
indake gualanirangko daka. Iano chu-gimik tense systemko nigija, 
jajaani aro gualani dongrongbatgipa bakrangkosan niaia. Gipin 
bakko talatmitingo nangchapatgipakode iano on-taija. 
“Chinga ambin anti Guwahatichi re-angenga” aro “We are 
going to Guwahati next week”, ia sentencegnion “re-angenga” 
aro “are going” Verbrang present progressive tenseo ong-a aro 
daka-ka-anini ong-engmitingko mesokgipa “-eng” aro “-ing” 
chinrang donga. Indiba ia daka-ka-ania da-o ong-enggipa ong-ja; 
mikkangchi ong-nasienggipasa ine “ambin anti” aro “next week” 
adverbrang mesoka. Indake, “-gen” ingipasan mikkangchi dakna- 
ka-nagipako mesokgipa ong-aija ine bi-sarangko ma-siatna nanga. 
Englisho past tenseko batanggimin somoeko mesokna aro 
mande ra-aniko mesokna jakkala. “Did you visit your old school?” 
inode batanggimin somoeko mesoka, indiba “Could you lend 
me some money?” inode, mande ra-e, bi-enggipa an-tangko onate, 
mol-molanisa ong-a. Englisho batanggimin somoena jakkalgipa 
Verbrang dakenggipa kamko matchotako mesoka. A-chikkuo iako 
“-ha” chinchi mesoka. "-jok” inode kam matchotako mesokoba, 
148

dakdaldalko ba matchota ru-utgijaniko mesoka. “chim” inode 
mande ra-ani orto donga baksana, “maiba dongkuenga” ingipa 
gisikko nakatata. Jekai: (i) Gari su-galahachim (indiba talkuja). 
(ii) Gari su-galjokchim (do-o ki-dapjok). Bi-sarang ia 
dingtanggrikanirangko nie ma-sie ra-na gita skigipa uamangna 
ranta ka-na cholko on-na nanga. 
Badita somoena (for + period of time) aro badia somoeoni 
(since + point of time), iarangko seanio jajaani aro gualani 
nakatronga. Badita somoena-ko on e Present Perfect Progressive 
Tenseo sentence rikon, *“I have been working here since two 
years” ine segualaniko nika. Grammarni gita ia seani tik ongja. 
Badita somoena-ko mesokani gimin, “I have been working here 
for two years” ine aganosa/ seosa sentence tik ong-a. Badia 
somoeoni ko mesokna Achikkuo “Anga iano 1960 onin 
dongenga” ba “Anga iano 1960 onin dongengaha” ine agana/sea. 
Iako ja-rike, Englisho * “I am staying here since 1960” ine 
aganode/ seode gualani ong-a. “I have been staying here since 
1960” inosa sentence tik ong-a. Sentencerangko nitaipilode iako 
nika: A-chikkuo badia somoeoniko mesokon, sentenceko present 
progressive ba past progressive tenseo rikna man-a. Indiba 
Englishode indake ong ja; sentenceko present perfect progressive 
tenseo rikosa tik ong-a. 
Englisho verb present perfect tenseo ong-ode, yesterday 
(mejalo), last week (meja antio), last year (meja bilsio) ingipa 
batanggimin somoeko mesokgipa adverbrangko jakkalja. Past 
Perfect Progressive Tensekode A chikkuo jakkalrongja. Sentence 
rikdrana man-oba, aganbewalode indaka sentencerangko 
jakkalja. English skie ra-enggiparang ka-mao on-gipako gisik 
ra-ode gualani dongjawa: 
Verb Simple Past + yesterday / last week 
Verb Present Perfect + today / this week 
Verb Simple Present + everyday / every month 
Jekai : “He went yesterday”. 
“He has gone today”. 
“He goes every month”.

Gisik ra-na dakchakgipa cholrangko man-a dipet jakkalo 
nama.

Aganako Baibai Aganpaani aro Aganskaani: 
Aganako Baibai Aganpaaniko Direct Narration aro 
Aganskaaniko Indirect Narration ine English Grammaro agana. 
Ia aganani bewalko Englisho donjujagrikna nangon, bi-sarang 
gualanirangko dakronga. Gualaniranga verbrangko dingtang 
dingtang bimangrango jakkalna nangaoni nakata. Sentencesako 
mesokani gita ra-na: “Tom ki-tap kingsako nanga ine aganaha”. 
Ia sentenceni ja-pang (main) clauseni verb “aganaha” past tenseo 
ong-a; indiba pangchakgipa (dependent ) clauseni verb “nanga” 
present tenseo ong-a. Ja-pang clauseni verb past tenseo ong-ode, 
pangchakgipa clauseni verb-ba past tenseo ong-pana nangaia. 
On-sogimin sentence Englisho indake ong-gen: “Tom said that 
he wanted a book”, “said” aro “wanted” verb ge-gnian past tenseo 
ong-a. A-chikkuni bilmanani gimin bang-a bi-sarangan ia 
sentenceko indake seguala; * "Tom said that he wants a book”. 
Kosako on-gipa niamko ja-rikdugae segualaniko niskana. The 
teacher said, “The sun rises in the east.” Tense apsan ong-na nanga 
inani gimin, iako indake sesretskaa: * "The teacher said that the 
sun rose in the east”. Salara jringnan salaramchini naa, 
gipinchipaknide ong-ja. Indake jringnan apsangipa ba kakket 
ong-giparangnade aganskaanirangoba present tenseon seaia. Uni 
gimin sentenceara indakesa ong-gen: “The teacher said that the 
sun rises in the east”. 
Ge-etani donggipa sentenceo rokom gipin gualaniko 
nikrongskaa. “My father said to me, ‘Get me a book’ ” ingipa 
sentenceko nina. Ia sentenceko Aganskaanio donode indake 
ong-a: “My father asked me to get him a book". Indiba baibai 
agangipaon “to” dongjani gimin bi-sarang ia sentenceko indake 
sesreta: * "My father asked me get a book”. 
“Wh”-chi a-bachenggipa kattako baibai agananio jakkala 
dongoba bi-sarang gualaniko dakronga. “wh” kattaranga who, 
whom, whose, what, why, when, where aro which. Ia kattarangchi

a-bachenggipa clauserangko wh-clauserang ine agana. ‘Jane asked 
me, *Where did you go?’ ” Iako bi-sarang, * “Jane asked me 
where did I go”, ine seguala. Ian mingsa niamko name ma-sijani 
a-sel ong-a. Englisho wh-clause gipin clauseni bak ong-ode, wh- 
clauseni subjectko verbna skang sea. Jekai: (i) She asked me 
where I learnt painting, (ii) Do you know where he works? 
150 
Subject “I”, "learnt” verbna skang aro subject “he” “works” 
verbna skang re-a. Niamko name talate on-ode bi-saran* 
mi alaniran alrn Halriattro kn n • 1 ® 
dakmitingo mingsako gisik ra-na nanggen. Wh-chi a-bachenggipa 
sentence gipin sentenceni bak ong-gija jakgitelgipa sentence 
ong-ode, verb tong-sa subjectni mikkango aro tong-sara subjectni 
ja-mano ong-a. Jekai: “What are you doing?” Verb ge-sasan 
ong-aiode, ua verb subectni mikkangon ong-aia. Jekai: “Where is 
my book?” Ia obostaode wh-clauseo gita subjectko skang done, 
* “What you are doing?” ine seode ong-skaja. 
Active Voice aro Passive Voice 
Maikoba dakgipa-ka gipaan sentenceni subject ong-ode, ua 
sentence Active Voiceo ong-a. Indiba dakako -ka-ako man-gipasa 
sentenceni subject ong-ode, ua sentence Passive Voiceo ong-a. 
Passive Voiceoba subject-verb agreement ba subject aro verbni 
“Number”-o meligrikani dongna nanga. Sentenceo dakgipa- 
ka-gipa -ko mesokna nangode, Englisho “by-phrase”- ko jakkala. 
Mesokanirangko nina: 
Active Voice: He has watered the plants. 
Passive Voice: The plants have been watered by him. 
Active Voiceo, subject third person singular number ong-ani 
gimin, “has”-ko jakkala. Passive Voiceo, daka-ka ako man-gipa 
“plants”-sa subject ong-skaani gimin aro uara bang-gipa munbero 
ong-ani gimin, “has”- ni pal “have” -ko jakkala. A-chikkuo number 
aro personna verbni bimang dingtang ong-jani gimin, indaka 
sentenceo bi-sarang skanggipa “has”-an Passive Voiceoba 
cholikuaigen ine ma-sisreta. 
Ia Passive Voiceo, by-phraseara “by him” ong-a. Iana

jajrengani A-chikkuo dongja. Beben A-chikkuo Passive Voiceo 
sentence rikna man-a; indiba uah rikdraanisan ong-aia. 
Aganrongbewalo A-chikkuo Passive Voiceko jakkalrongja. 
Englisho nengrabee, "Oh, it has already been done by him , ine 
aganaia. Ia sentence Passive Voice ong-a aro sentenceni vetbara 
be + past participle ong-a. A-chikkuode iako “Bian dakmanAha 
ine Active Voiceo aganaia. Passive Voiceko jakkale, O, uko 
bichin dakmanaha” inode aganrongbewal ong-ja, knadikata aro 
ortoba srangja. Indake Passive Voiceko A-chikkuo jakkalrongjani 
151 
gimin, an-chingni bi-sarang Englisho Voiceko skie ra-anio 
neng-nika. 
Minggipin jajaaniko Passive Voiceo agangipa segipa 
sentenceko A-chikkuo donskaanio nikronga. Passive Voiceo “is 
washed” ine seako nikode, “is” ko nikchange, “washed” -ko niari 
“su-galaha” ine donskaa. Indiba “is” present tenseosa ong-ani 
gimin A-chikkuo “su-gala” inesa ong-a; “was washed” inosa 
“su-galaha” ong-gen. lako changsa name talate on-ode bi sarang 
gisik ra-aigen. 
Bon-atani 
Ku-gitalko skie ra-giparang Ku-amani bilmananichi Ku-gitalo 
seanio/ agananio gualanirangko daka ine nikmanaha. Iana agreba 
bi-sarang gipin a selrangni gimin Ku-gitalko skie ra-anio 
neng-nikanirangko chagronga. Ua a-selrangoni mitamara iarang 
ong-naba donga: skie ra-enggipani gisik nangani komiani, 
skienggipani skianina gisik on-gijani aro ku-gipinni grammarko 
uisranggijani, skigipa an-tangan grammarko name ma-sigijani, 
onkalgipa classrang-o bi-sani name skianiko man-gijani, skigipani 
skiani chol-bewalrango skisame on-aniko man-gijani. Dingtang 
dingtang Ku-sikrang maidake re-a aro kam ka-a uko uigiparang, 
tosusagrike skianichi bi-sarangko ma siatna man-a. 
Ku-sikrangni Grammarko pe-rarone tosusagrike nianichi, 
bi-sarangni gualgnirangko niksamsoe uisamsoe ra-aniara, skie 
ra-ani aro skiani kam minggnikon nengrabatata. Gualanirang 
gimikko champengna man-jaoba, uarangko komiatna man-a.

Iandakachi, skie ra-enggipa aro skigipako somoe apjokata. Un 
baksana, niksamsogipa gualanirango pangchake, gualanirangko 
namatpilna ku-sikchi ba see ranta ka-anirangko a-same tarie donna 
man-a.
